    if (Test-AlreadyInstalled -Dest $dest -Marker $marker) {
        Write-Log "skip $($Tool.id) $marker"
        return $script:CachedRecord
    }
    $fresh = Join-Path $TmpDir ('files-' + $Tool.id)
    if (Test-Path $fresh) { Remove-Item $fresh -Recurse -Force }
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $fresh | Out-Null
    $hashes = @{}
    foreach ($name in $names) {
        $asset = @(ConvertTo-Array $release.assets | Where-Object { $_.name -eq $name })
        if ($asset.Count -ne 1) { throw "$($Tool.id) release is missing $name" }
        $file = Join-Path $fresh $name
        Write-Log "get $($Tool.id) $($release.tag_name) $name"
        Save-Url -Url $asset[0].browser_download_url -Dest $file
        $actual = Get-Sha256Hex $file
        $published = Get-PublishedHash -Release $release -AssetName $name
        if ($published -and $published.hash -ne $actual) {
            throw "$($Tool.id) $name hash mismatch"
        }
        $hashes[$name] = $actual
    }
    Move-FlattenedStage -Stage $fresh -Dest $dest
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $fresh -Force
    $shims = Install-Shims -Tool $Tool -Dest $dest
    Write-Marker -Dest $dest -Marker $marker
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        id = $Tool.id
        version = [string]$release.tag_name
        asset = ($names -join '+')
        sha256 = $hashes
        hash_source = 'per-file'
        dir = Get-RelativeKitPath $dest
        shims = $shims
    }
}

function Install-RenderDocTool {
    param($Tool)
    $release = Get-GitHubRelease -Repo $Tool.repo -Release $null
    $url = Get-RenderDocZipUrl -Tag ([string]$release.tag_name)
    $dest = Get-ToolDirectory $Tool.id
    $newer = Get-NewerReleaseRecord -Tool $Tool -Directory $dest -Version $release.tag_name
    if ($newer) { return $newer }
    $marker = [string]$release.tag_name
    if (Test-AlreadyInstalled -Dest $dest -Marker $marker) {
        Write-Log "skip $($Tool.id) $marker"
        return $script:CachedRecord
    }
    $zip = Join-Path $TmpDir ("RenderDoc_" + $release.tag_name + "_64.zip")
    Write-Log "get renderdoc $url"
    Save-Url -Url $url -Dest $zip
    $actual = Get-Sha256Hex $zip
    Expand-FlattenedZip -Zip $zip -Dest $dest
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $zip -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    $shims = Install-Shims -Tool $Tool -Dest $dest
    Write-Marker -Dest $dest -Marker $marker
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        id = $Tool.id
        version = [string]$release.tag_name
        asset = $url
        sha256 = $actual
        hash_source = 'computed'
        dir = Get-RelativeKitPath $dest
        shims = $shims
    }
}

function Test-DotNet10 {
    param([string]$Executable)
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Executable -PathType Leaf)) { return $false }
    $prev = $ErrorActionPreference
    try {
        $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
        $sdks = @(& $Executable --list-sdks 2>&1)
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0 -or -not ($sdks -match '^10\.\d+\.\d+\s+\[')) { return $false }
        $runtimes = @(& $Executable --list-runtimes 2>&1)
        return ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0 -and [bool]($runtimes -match '^Microsoft\.NETCore\.App 10\.\d+\.\d+\s+\['))
    } catch { return $false }
    finally { $ErrorActionPreference = $prev }
}

function Find-DotNet10 {
    $candidates = @()
    foreach ($root in @($env:DOTNET_ROOT_X64, $env:DOTNET_ROOT)) {
        if ($root) { $candidates += Join-Path $root 'dotnet.exe' }
    }
    $command = Get-Command dotnet -CommandType Application -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($command) { $candidates += $command.Source }
    foreach ($root in @($env:ProgramW6432, $env:ProgramFiles)) {
        if ($root) { $candidates += Join-Path $root 'dotnet\dotnet.exe' }
    }
    foreach ($candidate in @($candidates | Select-Object -Unique)) {
        if (Test-DotNet10 $candidate) { return $candidate }
    }
    return $null
}

function Initialize-DotNet10 {
    $found = Find-DotNet10
    if (-not $found) {
        $winget = Get-Command winget -CommandType Application -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        if (-not $winget) { throw 'This kit requires a .NET 10 SDK and runtime for ilspycmd; winget is unavailable. Install Microsoft.DotNet.SDK.10, then rerun the installer.' }
        Write-Log 'installing Microsoft.DotNet.SDK.10 via winget'
        $prev = $ErrorActionPreference
        try {
            $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
            & $winget.Source install --id Microsoft.DotNet.SDK.10 -e --accept-package-agreements --accept-source-agreements --disable-interactivity | ForEach-Object { Write-Log -Detail "winget: $_" }
            $code = $LASTEXITCODE
        } finally { $ErrorActionPreference = $prev }
        if ($code -ne 0) { throw "winget Microsoft.DotNet.SDK.10 failed ($code)" }
        $found = Find-DotNet10
        if (-not $found) { throw 'Microsoft.DotNet.SDK.10 completed but a stable .NET 10 SDK and runtime were not found. Check its installation and rerun.' }
    }
    $script:DotNetExe = $found
    $script:DotNetHome = Split-Path -Parent $found
    return $found
}

function Install-DotNetTool {
    param($Tool)
    $dotnet = Initialize-DotNet10
    $dest = Get-ToolDirectory $Tool.id
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dest | Out-Null
    if ((Test-AlreadyInstalled -Dest $dest -Marker 'present') -and (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $dest -Filter ($Tool.shims[0].exe_glob) -Recurse -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
        Write-Log "skip $($Tool.id)"
        return $script:CachedRecord
    }
    $prev = $ErrorActionPreference
    $versionArgs = if ($Tool.package_version) { @('--version', $Tool.package_version) } else { @() }
    try {
        $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
        & $dotnet tool install $Tool.package --tool-path $dest --framework net10.0 @versionArgs | ForEach-Object { Write-Log -Detail "dotnet: $_" }
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            & $dotnet tool update $Tool.package --tool-path $dest --framework net10.0 @versionArgs | ForEach-Object { Write-Log -Detail "dotnet: $_" }
        }
        $code = $LASTEXITCODE
    } finally { $ErrorActionPreference = $prev }
    if ($code -ne 0) { throw "dotnet tool $($Tool.package) failed ($code)" }
    $packageRows = @(& $dotnet tool list --tool-path $dest)
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "dotnet tool list failed ($LASTEXITCODE): $($Tool.package)" }
    $versionRow = @($packageRows | Where-Object { $_ -match ('^' + [regex]::Escape($Tool.package) + '\s+') })
    if ($versionRow.Count -ne 1) { throw "cannot read installed version: $($Tool.package)" }
    $packageVersion = ($versionRow[0] -split '\s+')[1]
    $shims = Install-Shims -Tool $Tool -Dest $dest
    Write-Marker -Dest $dest -Marker 'present'
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        id = $Tool.id
        version = $packageVersion
        asset = [string]$Tool.package
        sha256 = $null
        hash_source = 'nuget'
        dir = Get-RelativeKitPath $dest
        shims = $shims
    }
}

function Install-PipVenv {
    param($Tool)
    $basePython = Initialize-SharedPython
    $dest = Get-ToolDirectory $Tool.id
    $exe = Join-Path $dest 'Scripts\python.exe'
    if ((Test-AlreadyInstalled -Dest $dest -Marker 'present') -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $exe)) {
        Write-Log "skip $($Tool.id)"
        return $script:CachedRecord
    }
    $prev = $ErrorActionPreference
    $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
    & $basePython -m venv $dest | ForEach-Object { Write-Log -Detail "python: $_" }
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { $ErrorActionPreference = $prev; throw "venv failed ($LASTEXITCODE)" }
    $pyexe = Join-Path $dest 'Scripts\python.exe'
    $packageSpec = if ($Tool.package_version) { $Tool.package + '==' + $Tool.package_version } else { $Tool.package }
    & $pyexe -m pip install --upgrade --disable-pip-version-check $packageSpec | ForEach-Object { Write-Log -Detail "pip: $_" }
    $code = $LASTEXITCODE
    $ErrorActionPreference = $prev
    if ($code -ne 0) { throw "pip install $($Tool.package) failed ($code)" }
    $packageVersion = (& $pyexe -c "import importlib.metadata; print(importlib.metadata.version('$($Tool.package)'))" | Out-String).Trim()
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0 -or -not $packageVersion) { throw "cannot read installed version: $($Tool.package)" }
    $shims = Install-Shims -Tool $Tool -Dest $dest
    Write-Marker -Dest $dest -Marker 'present'
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        id = $Tool.id
        version = $packageVersion
        asset = [string]$Tool.package
        sha256 = $null
        hash_source = 'pypi'
        dir = Get-RelativeKitPath $dest
        shims = $shims
    }
}

# Reuse a matching global REA without taking ownership or changing its settings.
function Get-ExternalRea {
    param($Tool)
    if ($Tool.id -ne 'rea' -or $Force) { return $null }
    $unmanaged = @()
    foreach ($command in @(Get-Command rea.cmd -All -CommandType Application -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
        if ($command.Source.StartsWith($BinDir.TrimEnd('\') + '\', [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { continue }
        $version = (& $command.Source --version 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
        $match = [regex]::Match($version, '(?<![0-9.])(\d+\.\d+\.\d+)(?![0-9.])')
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0 -or -not $match.Success) { $unmanaged += $command.Source; continue }
        $comparison = Compare-ToolVersion $match.Value $Tool.package_version
        if ($null -eq $comparison -or $comparison -lt 0 -or ([version]$match.Value).Major -ne ([version]$Tool.package_version).Major) { $unmanaged += ($command.Source + ' version ' + $match.Value); continue }
        $nodeHome = if ($script:Node) { Split-Path -Parent $script:Node.node } else { $null }
        $shim = Write-Shim -Name 'rea' -Target $command.Source -NodeHome $nodeHome
        return [pscustomobject]@{ id='rea'; version=$match.Value; source='existing-path'; owned=$false; command=$command.Source; command_sha256=(Get-Sha256Hex $command.Source); dir=$null; shims=@{rea=(Get-RelativeKitPath $shim)} }
    }
    if ($unmanaged.Count) { throw "Existing unowned REA needs its owner's update or compatibility verification: $($unmanaged -join ', '). It was preserved; no duplicate was installed. A verified owner tool receipt can be supplied with -ToolDirectories." }
    return $null
}

function Assert-NpmIntegrity {
    param($Tool, [string]$Dest)
    if (-not $Tool.npm_integrity) { return }
    $text = [IO.File]::ReadAllText((Join-Path $Dest 'package-lock.json'))
    # npm has an empty root key; PS5.1's PSCustomObject converter rejects it.
    if ($PSVersionTable.PSVersion.Major -ge 6) { $lock = ConvertFrom-Json -InputObject $text -AsHashtable }
    else {
        Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Web.Extensions
        $json = New-Object System.Web.Script.Serialization.JavaScriptSerializer
        $json.MaxJsonLength=16777216
        $lock=$json.DeserializeObject($text)
    }
    $row = $lock['packages']['node_modules/' + $Tool.package]
    if (-not $row -or $row['version'] -ne $Tool.package_version -or $row['integrity'] -cne $Tool.npm_integrity) { throw "npm integrity/version mismatch: $($Tool.package)" }
}

function Install-NpmTool {
    param($Tool)
    $dest = Get-ToolDirectory $Tool.id
    if (Test-AlreadyInstalled -Dest $dest -Marker 'present') { return $script:CachedRecord }
    $node = Initialize-SharedNode ($Tool.id -eq 'rea')
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dest | Out-Null
    $prev = $ErrorActionPreference
    $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
    $savedNodePath = $env:PATH
    try {
        $env:PATH = (Split-Path -Parent $node.node) + ';' + $env:PATH
        & $node.npm install --prefix $dest --no-audit --no-fund --ignore-scripts --engine-strict ($Tool.package + '@' + $(if ($Tool.package_version) { $Tool.package_version } else { 'latest' })) | ForEach-Object { Write-Log -Detail "npm: $_" }
    } finally { $env:PATH = $savedNodePath }
    $code = $LASTEXITCODE
    $ErrorActionPreference = $prev
    if ($code -ne 0) { throw "npm install $($Tool.package) failed ($code)" }
    $packageJson = Join-Path $dest ('node_modules/' + $Tool.package + '/package.json')
    $packageVersion = (Get-Content -LiteralPath $packageJson -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json).version
    if (-not $packageVersion) { throw "cannot read installed version: $($Tool.package)" }
    Assert-NpmIntegrity -Tool $Tool -Dest $dest
    $shims = Install-Shims -Tool $Tool -Dest $dest
    Write-Marker -Dest $dest -Marker 'present'
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        npm_integrity = $Tool.npm_integrity
        id = $Tool.id; version = $packageVersion; asset = $Tool.package
        sha256 = $null; hash_source = 'npm-lock-integrity'
        dir = Get-RelativeKitPath $dest; shims = $shims
    }
}

function New-SpacelessDirectory {
    $bases = @()
    if ($env:TEMP -and $env:TEMP -notmatch ' ') { $bases += $env:TEMP }
    $winTemp = Join-Path $env:SystemRoot 'Temp'
    if ($winTemp -notmatch ' ') { $bases += $winTemp }
    foreach ($base in $bases) {
        $dir = Join-Path $base ('rt7z' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
        try {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null
            return $dir
        } catch { }
    }
    throw '7-Zip installer needs a temp directory with no spaces'
}

function Invoke-SevenZip {
    param([string]$SevenZip, [string[]]$ArgumentList)
    $prev = $ErrorActionPreference
    $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
    & $SevenZip @ArgumentList | ForEach-Object { Write-Log -Detail "7z: $_" }
    $code = $LASTEXITCODE
    $ErrorActionPreference = $prev
    if ($code -ne 0) { throw "7z failed ($code)" }
}

function Expand-MsiAdmin {
    param([string]$Msi, [string]$Dest)
    $stage = New-SpacelessDirectory
    $extract = Join-Path $stage 'out'
    $localMsi = Join-Path $stage 'pkg.msi'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $extract | Out-Null
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath $Msi -Destination $localMsi
    try {
        $msiexec = Join-Path $env:SystemRoot 'System32\msiexec.exe'
        $prev = $ErrorActionPreference
        $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
        $proc = Start-Process -FilePath $msiexec -ArgumentList @('/a', $localMsi, '/qn', ('TARGETDIR=' + $extract)) -WindowStyle Hidden -Wait -PassThru
        $code = $proc.ExitCode
        $ErrorActionPreference = $prev
        if ($code -ne 0 -and $code -ne 3010) { throw "msiexec /a failed ($code)" }
        $payload = Join-Path $extract 'Files\7-Zip'
        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $payload '7z.exe')) -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $payload '7z.dll'))) {
            throw '7-Zip MSI did not contain 7z.exe and 7z.dll'
        }
        Move-FlattenedStage -Stage $payload -Dest $Dest
    } finally {
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $stage) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $stage -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
    }
}

function Get-SevenZip {
    $exe = Join-Path (Get-ToolDirectory '7zip') '7z.exe'
    $dll = Join-Path (Get-ToolDirectory '7zip') '7z.dll'
    if ((Test-Path -LiteralPath $exe) -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dll)) { return $exe }
    $catalog = Get-Catalog
    $tool = ConvertTo-Array $catalog.tools | Where-Object { $_.id -eq '7zip' } | Select-Object -First 1
    if (-not $tool) { throw 'catalog has no 7zip tool' }
    if ([string]$tool.unpack -ne 'msi-admin') { throw '7zip tool must unpack with msi-admin' }
    $parentTool = $script:CurrentTool
    $parentCached = $script:CachedRecord
    $parentBackup = $script:PromotedBackup
    try { $row = Install-One $tool } finally {
        $script:CurrentTool = $parentTool
        $script:CachedRecord = $parentCached
        $script:PromotedBackup = $parentBackup
    }
    if ($row) { $script:ExtraInstalled += @($row) }
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $exe) -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dll)) {
        throw '7zip install did not produce 7z.exe and 7z.dll'
    }
    return $exe
}

function Expand-WithSevenZip {
    param([string]$Archive, [string]$Dest)
    $seven = Get-SevenZip
    $stage = Join-Path $TmpDir ('stage-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $stage | Out-Null
    try {
        Invoke-SevenZip -SevenZip $seven -ArgumentList @('x', '-y', "-o$stage", $Archive)
        Move-FlattenedStage -Stage $stage -Dest $Dest
    } finally {
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $stage) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $stage -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
    }
}

function Expand-NsisApp64 {
    param([string]$Installer, [string]$Dest)
    $seven = Get-SevenZip
    $outer = Join-Path $TmpDir ('nsis-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
    $innerStage = Join-Path $TmpDir ('app64-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $outer, $innerStage | Out-Null
    try {
        Invoke-SevenZip -SevenZip $seven -ArgumentList @('e', '-y', "-o$outer", $Installer, '$PLUGINSDIR\app-64.7z')
        $packed = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $outer -Filter 'app-64.7z' -File)
        if ($packed.Count -ne 1) { throw "nsis app-64.7z count $($packed.Count)" }
        Invoke-SevenZip -SevenZip $seven -ArgumentList @('x', '-y', "-o$innerStage", $packed[0].FullName)
        $payload = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $innerStage -Filter 'LDtk.exe' -File)
        if ($payload.Count -ne 1) { throw "LDtk.exe count $($payload.Count)" }
        Move-FlattenedStage -Stage $innerStage -Dest $Dest
    } finally {
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $outer) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $outer -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $innerStage) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $innerStage -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
    }
}

function Install-GitHubArchive {
    param($Tool)
    $repo = [string]$Tool.repo
    if ($Tool.ref -eq 'release') {
        $release = Get-GitHubRelease -Repo $repo -Release $Tool.release
        $refName = [string]$release.tag_name
        $url = Get-GitHubArchiveUrl -Repo $repo -Kind 'tag' -Ref $refName
        $marker = 'tag|' + $refName
        $version = $refName
    } else {
        $meta = Invoke-GitHubApi -ApiPath ("repos/" + $repo)
        $branch = [string]$meta.default_branch
        if (-not $branch) { throw "$($Tool.id) has no default branch" }
        $commit = Invoke-GitHubApi -ApiPath ("repos/" + $repo + "/commits/" + $branch)
        $sha = [string]$commit.sha
        if ($sha -notmatch '^[0-9a-f]{40}$') { throw "$($Tool.id) commit sha was not a sha" }
        $url = Get-GitHubArchiveUrl -Repo $repo -Kind 'commit' -Ref $sha
        $marker = 'commit|' + $sha
        $version = $sha.Substring(0, 12)
    }
    $dest = Get-ToolDirectory $Tool.id
    if (Test-AlreadyInstalled -Dest $dest -Marker $marker) {
        Write-Log "skip $($Tool.id) $marker"
        return $script:CachedRecord
    }
    $zip = Join-Path $TmpDir ($Tool.id + '-src.zip')
    Write-Log "get $($Tool.id) $url"
    Save-Url -Url $url -Dest $zip
    $actual = Get-Sha256Hex $zip
    Expand-FlattenedZip -Zip $zip -Dest $dest
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $zip -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    $shims = Install-Shims -Tool $Tool -Dest $dest
    Write-Marker -Dest $dest -Marker $marker
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        id = $Tool.id
        version = $version
        asset = $url
        sha256 = $actual
        hash_source = 'computed'
        dir = Get-RelativeKitPath $dest
        shims = $shims
    }
}

function Install-FighterFactory {
    param($Tool)
    $url = Resolve-FighterFactoryZipUrl
    $name = [IO.Path]::GetFileName(([Uri]$url).AbsolutePath)
    if ($name -notmatch '^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+\.zip$') { throw "bad fighter factory file name $name" }
    $dest = Get-ToolDirectory $Tool.id
    if (Test-AlreadyInstalled -Dest $dest -Marker $url) {
        Write-Log "skip $($Tool.id) $url"
        return $script:CachedRecord
    }
    $zip = Join-Path $TmpDir $name
    Write-Log "get fighter-factory $url"
    Save-Url -Url $url -Dest $zip
    $actual = Get-Sha256Hex $zip
    Expand-FlattenedZip -Zip $zip -Dest $dest
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $zip -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    $shims = Install-Shims -Tool $Tool -Dest $dest
    Write-Marker -Dest $dest -Marker $url
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        id = $Tool.id
        version = $name
        asset = $url
        sha256 = $actual
        hash_source = 'computed'
        dir = Get-RelativeKitPath $dest
        shims = $shims
    }
}

function Select-InstallTools {
    param($Selected, $Catalog)
    $groupIds = @('capa-rules', 'capa-source', 'capa')
    $groupAdded = $false
    foreach ($tool in (ConvertTo-Array $Selected)) {
        if ($tool.id -notin $groupIds) { $tool; continue }
        if ($groupAdded) { continue }
        $parent = @(ConvertTo-Array $Catalog.tools | Where-Object { $_.id -eq 'capa' })
        if ($parent.Count -ne 1) { throw 'The capa dependency group requires exactly one capa catalog entry.' }
        $parent[0]
        $groupAdded = $true
    }
}

function Install-One {
    param($Tool)
    $script:CurrentTool = $Tool
    $dest = Get-ToolDirectory $Tool.id
    # Explicit no-update mode reuses valid bytes before any release or registry lookup.
    if ($NoUpdate -and -not $Tool.requires -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dest '.kit-ok'))) {
        if ($Tool.needs_jdk -and -not $script:JavaHome) { $script:JavaHome = Install-Jdk -Major ([int]$Tool.needs_jdk) }
        if ($Tool.method -eq 'dotnet-tool') { Initialize-DotNet10 | Out-Null }
        $marker = if ($Tool.id -in @('capa-rules','capa-source') -and $Tool.release) { 'tag|' + $Tool.release } else { (Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dest '.kit-ok') -Raw).Trim() }
        if (Test-AlreadyInstalled -Dest $dest -Marker $marker) { return $script:CachedRecord }
    }
    Resolve-PackageVersion $Tool
    $capaTag = $null
    if ($Tool.id -eq 'capa') {
        $currentCapa = Join-Path $dest '.kit-record.json'
        $current = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $currentCapa) { Get-Content -LiteralPath $currentCapa -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json } else { $null }
        $capaTag = if ($NoUpdate -and $current.version -match '^v?\d+\.\d+\.\d+$') { 'v' + $current.version.TrimStart('v') } else { [string](Get-GitHubRelease -Repo 'mandiant/capa').tag_name }
        if ($capaTag -notmatch '^v\d+\.\d+\.\d+$') { throw "invalid capa release version: $capaTag" }
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $currentCapa) {
            if ((Compare-ToolVersion $current.version $capaTag) -gt 0) { $capaTag = 'v' + $current.version.TrimStart('v') }
        }
        $Tool | Add-Member -NotePropertyName package_version -NotePropertyValue $capaTag.Substring(1) -Force
    }
    Write-Log -Detail "resolved $($Tool.id): method=$($Tool.method) directory=$dest force=$Force"
    Assert-OwnedToolPath $dest
    $savedShims = @{}
    foreach ($shim in (ConvertTo-Array $Tool.shims)) {
        if ($shim.name -notmatch '^[A-Za-z0-9-]+$') { throw "unsafe shim name: $($shim.name)" }
        $shimPath = Join-Path $BinDir ($shim.name + '.cmd')
        $savedShims[$shimPath] = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $shimPath) { [IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($shimPath) } else { $null }
    }
    $packageBackup = $null
    $parentStarted = $false
    $dependencyChanges = @()
    $previousExtra = @($script:ExtraInstalled)
    try {
    $external = if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dest)) { Get-ExternalRea -Tool $Tool } else { $null }
    if ($external) { return $external }
    foreach ($id in (ConvertTo-Array $Tool.requires)) {
        $dependency = @(ConvertTo-Array (Get-Catalog).tools | Where-Object { $_.id -eq $id })
        if ($dependency.Count -ne 1) { throw "unknown dependency: $id" }
        $change = $null
        if ($capaTag) {
            $dependency[0] | Add-Member -NotePropertyName release -NotePropertyValue $capaTag -Force
            $change = [pscustomobject]@{
                dir = Get-ToolDirectory $id
                existed = Test-Path -LiteralPath (Get-ToolDirectory $id)
                backup = $null
            }
            $dependencyChanges += $change
        }
        $script:PromotedBackup = $null
        try { $script:ExtraInstalled += @(Install-One $dependency[0]) }
        finally { if ($change) { $change.backup = $script:PromotedBackup } }
    }
    $script:CurrentTool = $Tool
    $script:CachedRecord = $null
    $script:PromotedBackup = $null
    $parentStarted = $true
    if ($Tool.method -eq 'dotnet-tool') { Initialize-DotNet10 | Out-Null }
    if ($Tool.method -in @('pip-venv', 'dotnet-tool', 'npm-tool') -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dest)) {
        if (Test-AlreadyInstalled -Dest $dest -Marker 'present') {
            $script:CachedRecord = Add-ToolReceipt -Tool $Tool -Row $script:CachedRecord -Directory $dest
            Write-AtomicJson -Path (Join-Path $dest '.kit-record.json') -Text ($script:CachedRecord | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8)
            return $script:CachedRecord
        }
        $backupCandidate = Join-Path $Sandbox ('backups/' + $Tool.id + '-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Split-Path -Parent $backupCandidate) | Out-Null
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $dest -Destination $backupCandidate -Recurse
        $packageBackup = $backupCandidate
    }
    $row = switch ($Tool.method) {
        'github-release' { Install-GitHubZipTool $Tool }
        'github-files' { Install-GitHubFilesTool $Tool }
        'renderdoc-stable-zip' { Install-RenderDocTool $Tool }
        'dotnet-tool' { Install-DotNetTool $Tool }
        'pip-venv' { Install-PipVenv $Tool }
        'npm-tool' { Install-NpmTool $Tool }
        'github-archive' { Install-GitHubArchive $Tool }
        'virtualltek-portable' { Install-FighterFactory $Tool }
        'local-script' { Install-LocalScriptTool $Tool }
        default { throw "unknown method $($Tool.method)" }
    }
    if ($row -is [array] -or -not $row -or $row.id -ne $Tool.id) { throw "invalid install result: $($Tool.id)" }
    Assert-ToolPayload -Tool $Tool -Dest $dest
    $row = Add-ToolReceipt -Tool $Tool -Row $row -Directory $dest
    Write-AtomicJson -Path (Join-Path $dest '.kit-record.json') -Text ($row | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8)
    return $row
    } catch {
        $failure = $_
        $rollbackErrors = @()
        if ($capaTag) { $script:ExtraInstalled = $previousExtra }
        $rollback = if ($packageBackup) { $packageBackup } elseif ($parentStarted) { $script:PromotedBackup } else { $null }
        try {
            if ($rollback -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $rollback)) {
                Assert-OwnedToolPath $dest
                if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dest) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $dest -Recurse -Force }
                Move-Item -LiteralPath $rollback -Destination $dest
                Write-Log "restored last-good $($Tool.id)"
            }
        } catch { $rollbackErrors += $_.Exception.Message }
        for ($index = $dependencyChanges.Count - 1; $index -ge 0; $index--) {
            $change = $dependencyChanges[$index]
            try {
                Assert-OwnedToolPath $change.dir
                if ($change.backup -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $change.backup)) {
                    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $change.dir) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $change.dir -Recurse -Force }
                    Move-Item -LiteralPath $change.backup -Destination $change.dir
                } elseif (-not $change.existed -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $change.dir)) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $change.dir -Recurse -Force
                }
            } catch { $rollbackErrors += $_.Exception.Message }
        }
        foreach ($shimPath in $savedShims.Keys) {
            try {
                if ($null -ne $savedShims[$shimPath]) { [IO.File]::WriteAllBytes($shimPath, $savedShims[$shimPath]) }
                elseif (Test-Path -LiteralPath $shimPath) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $shimPath -Force }
            } catch { $rollbackErrors += $_.Exception.Message }
        }
        if ($rollbackErrors.Count) { throw "Install failed: $($failure.Exception.Message). Rollback incomplete: $($rollbackErrors -join '; ')" }
        throw $failure
    }
}

function Merge-ExtraInstalled {
    param($State)
    foreach ($extra in @($script:ExtraInstalled)) {
        if ($extra -and $extra.id) {
            $State.tools | Add-Member -NotePropertyName $extra.id -NotePropertyValue $extra -Force
        }
    }
    $script:ExtraInstalled = @()
}

if ($SelfTest) { Invoke-SelfTest }
if ($Plan) { Invoke-Plan }
if ($Force -and $NoUpdate) { throw 'Force and NoUpdate are mutually exclusive.' }

# All read/write state belongs to the existing owner; isolated roots never rewrite its shared pointer.
if ($Offline) { $Doctor = $true }
$KitRoot = Resolve-OwnerRoot -Source $SourceRoot -ExplicitRoot $InstallRoot -Pointer $PointerPath
$Sandbox = Join-Path $KitRoot 'sandbox'
$BinDir = Join-Path $Sandbox 'bin'
$ToolsDir = Join-Path $Sandbox 'tools'
$TmpDir = Join-Path $Sandbox 'tmp'
$LogDir = Join-Path $Sandbox 'log'
$LogPath = Join-Path $LogDir 'modkit.log'
$StatePath = Join-Path $Sandbox 'installed.json'
if ($InstallRoot) { $PointerPath = $null }
foreach ($path in @($KitRoot,$Sandbox,$BinDir,$ToolsDir,$TmpDir,$LogDir)) {
    if ((Test-Path -LiteralPath $path) -and ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $path -Force).Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint)) { throw "Linked owner state path refused: $path" }
}

$catalog = Get-Catalog
$selected = @(ConvertTo-Array $catalog.tools)
if ($Only) {
    $wanted = @($Only | ForEach-Object { $_.Split(',') } | ForEach-Object { $_.Trim().ToLowerInvariant() } | Where-Object { $_ })
    $unknown = @($wanted | Where-Object { $_ -notin $selected.id })
    if ($unknown.Count) { throw "unknown tool id: $($unknown -join ', ')" }
    $selected = @($selected | Where-Object { $wanted -contains $_.id })
    if ($selected.Count -eq 0) { throw "Only matched nothing: $($wanted -join ', ')" }
}
Initialize-ExternalTools $catalog
if ($Doctor) {
    $missing = 0
    $unverified = 0
    $state = New-State
    foreach ($tool in $selected) {
        try {
            $proof = ''
            $row = $state.tools.($tool.id)
            if (-not $row) { throw 'not installed' }
            foreach ($id in (ConvertTo-Array $tool.requires)) {
                if (-not $state.tools.$id) { throw "missing dependency: $id" }
                $dependency = $catalog.tools | Where-Object { $_.id -eq $id }
                Assert-ToolPayload -Tool $dependency -Dest (Get-ToolDirectory $id)
            }
            if ($row.source -eq 'existing-path' -and $row.owned -eq $false) {
                if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $row.command -PathType Leaf)) { throw 'external command missing' }
                if ($row.command_sha256 -and (Get-Sha256Hex $row.command) -ne $row.command_sha256) { throw 'external command hash changed' }
            } else {
                $toolDir = Get-ToolDirectory $tool.id
                Assert-ToolPayload -Tool $tool -Dest $toolDir
                # The kit executes these binaries against targets, so re-hash every file bound
                # in the install receipt; a changed one is never trusted as present.
                $receipt = @(if ($row.files) { $row.files.PSObject.Properties })
                if (-not $receipt.Count) { $proof = 'UNVERIFIED' }
                foreach ($file in $receipt) {
                    $path = Join-Path $toolDir $file.Name
                    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $path -PathType Leaf) -or (Get-Sha256Hex $path) -ne [string]$file.Value) {
                        Write-Host "CHANGED $($tool.id): $($file.Name) differs from its install receipt; reinstall with -Only $($tool.id) -Force"
                        throw 'changed after install'
                    }
                }
                if (-not $proof) { $proof = "verified $($receipt.Count) file(s)" }
            }
            foreach ($shim in (ConvertTo-Array $tool.shims)) {
                if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $BinDir ($shim.name + '.cmd')))) { throw "missing shim $($shim.name)" }
            }
            if ($proof -eq 'UNVERIFIED') {
                Write-Host "UNVERIFIED $($tool.id) $($row.version): no file receipt; reinstall with -Only $($tool.id) -Force to record one"
                $unverified++
            } else { Write-Host ("OK $($tool.id) $($row.version)" + $(if ($proof) { " ($proof)" } else { '' })) }
        } catch { Write-Host "FAIL $($tool.id): $($_.Exception.Message)"; $missing++ }
    }
    Write-Host 'Doctor re-hashes the files each install receipt binds (shimmed executables and key files) and checks the rest for presence; run the relevant tool on a lab sample for runtime proof.'
    if ($unverified) { Write-Host "$unverified tool(s) have no file receipt and were not digest-verified." }
    if ($missing) { exit 1 }
    exit 0
}

$selected = @(Select-InstallTools -Selected $selected -Catalog $catalog)
$directories = @($BinDir, $ToolsDir, $TmpDir, $LogDir)
if ($PointerPath) { $directories += $PointerDir }
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $directories | Out-Null
$lockPath = Join-Path $Sandbox '.install.lock'
try { $lock = [IO.File]::Open($lockPath, 'OpenOrCreate', 'ReadWrite', 'None') }
catch { throw 'Another installer is using this kit. Wait for it to finish.' }
try {
Write-Log "reverse-this $kitVersion  root=$KitRoot"
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $StatePath) {
    $stateBackup = $StatePath + '.pre-' + $kitVersion + '.json'
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $stateBackup)) { Copy-Item -LiteralPath $StatePath -Destination $stateBackup }
}
$state = New-State
$script:InstallState = $state
$state.kit_version = $kitVersion
$state.kit_root = $KitRoot
# Runtime owners get their patch updates even when every analysis package is already current.
$pythonTools = @($selected | Where-Object { $_.method -eq 'pip-venv' })
if ($pythonTools.Count) {
    $script:MinimumPython = if (@($pythonTools | Where-Object { $_.id -ne 'lief' }).Count) { [version]'3.10' } else { [version]'3.9' }
    Initialize-SharedPython | Out-Null
}
if (@($selected | Where-Object { $_.method -eq 'npm-tool' }).Count) { Initialize-SharedNode ($selected.id -contains 'rea') | Out-Null }
if (@($selected | Where-Object { $_.method -eq 'local-script' -and $_.python -eq 'shared' }).Count) { Initialize-SharedPython | Out-Null }
$ok = @()
$bad = @()
$index = 0
foreach ($tool in $selected) {
    $index += 1
    Write-Log ("[{0}/{1}] {2}" -f $index, $selected.Count, $tool.id)
    try {
        $row = Install-One $tool
        if ($row) {
            $state.tools | Add-Member -NotePropertyName $tool.id -NotePropertyValue $row -Force
        }
        if ($script:JavaHome) { $state.java_home = $script:JavaHome }
        Merge-ExtraInstalled $state
        Save-State $state
        $ok += $tool.id
    } catch {
        Write-Log "FAIL $($tool.id): $($_.Exception.Message)"
        $bad += $tool.id
        Merge-ExtraInstalled $state
        Save-State $state
    }
}
Write-Log ("done ok={0} failed={1}" -f $ok.Count, $bad.Count)
if ($bad.Count -gt 0) {
    Write-Log ("failed: " + ($bad -join ', '))
    Write-Log "log: $LogPath"
    exit 1
}
Write-Log "bin: $BinDir"
Write-Log "pointer: $PointerPath"
exit 0
} finally { $lock.Dispose() }

```

`README.md`:

```md
<p align="center">
  <img src="assets/mark.svg" width="72" height="72" alt="Reverse THIS!">
</p>

<div align="center">

# Reverse THIS!

<p>
  <a href="https://github.com/SenjuWoo/reverse-this/actions/workflows/ci.yml"><img src="https://github.com/SenjuWoo/reverse-this/actions/workflows/ci.yml/badge.svg" alt="CI"></a>
  <a href="LICENSE"><img src="https://img.shields.io/badge/license-MIT-33566E?labelColor=0d1117" alt="MIT License"></a>
  <a href="https://github.com/SenjuWoo/reverse-this/releases/latest"><img src="https://img.shields.io/github/v/release/SenjuWoo/reverse-this?label=release&color=33566E&labelColor=0d1117" alt="latest release"></a>
</p>

</div>

One click installs a Windows reverse-engineering and modding workbench with stable commands in `sandbox/bin`. It covers native and .NET apps, Android/Java bytecode, PyInstaller packages, binary formats, and game engines from Unreal and Unity to Source, REDengine, FromSoftware, GameMaker and Total War.

`sandbox` is a workspace folder, not process isolation. Tools run with your account's permissions. Use a separate VM or Windows Sandbox when a target requires isolation. This kit does not guarantee recovery of original source, a working build, missing assets, symbols or keys; decompilation does not change ownership or licensing.

No capability limit in this repository is permanent: each is scoped to a tool, version and mechanism in [encyclopedia/CLAIMS.md](encyclopedia/CLAIMS.md). The catalog is a convenience, not a permission system: a tool you supply in `sandbox/manual/<name>/` is usable without being in the catalog.

## Modular composition

Reverse THIS! is the optional reverse-engineering layer. It installs and runs alone; it does not require the Starter Bundle, Skyrim Forge, or any other forge, and none of them require it. Install only the layers you need:

| Layer | Owns | Works alone |
|---|---|---|
| [Ultimate AI Starter Bundle](https://github.com/SenjuWoo/Ultimate-AI-Starter-Bundle) | providers, shared compilers/runtimes, general skills, GitHub and work-hygiene routing, presentation | yes |
| Reverse THIS! (this repo) | REA, the analysis payload catalog, `sandbox/bin` shims, the target workspace and the encyclopedia | yes |
| [Skyrim Forge](https://github.com/SenjuWoo/SkyrimForge) and other forges | game-specific authoring, records, Papyrus, assets and game MCPs | yes |

The boundaries are deliberate:

- **One owner per tool.** This catalog has no Skyrim tools; xEdit, LOOT, the Creation Kit, houseCARL and Skyrim records stay with Forge. Forge may reference this kit's `sandbox/bin/7z.cmd` and DirectXTex tools instead of installing a second copy.
- **One shared runtime.** When the bundle's `UABS_PYTHON` is set, this installer reuses it. Otherwise it resolves one compatible base interpreter for its own use and never creates a second one beside a working installation. `UABS_TOOL_ROOTS` is honored.
- **Overlap is synced, not duplicated.** The installer detects a shared tool or runtime first: a current version is skipped, an older owned copy is updated in place, and a newer or unknown copy is preserved and reported. `-Force` is the only explicit reinstall; `-NoUpdate` keeps valid installed bytes. Shared payloads such as 7-Zip and DirectXTex are owned here, and another forge references them instead of installing a second copy.
- **No provider writes.** This installer never edits AI-provider settings, trust, model choices, MCP profiles or another owner's ledger.
- **No version coupling.** Each repository releases independently. The bundle's optional `reverse` pack downloads this repo's latest verified release at or above its floor and calls this installer; a Reverse THIS! release never requires a bundle release.
- **No hard dependency.** `install.cmd` works on a clean Windows machine; `-InstallRoot` creates an isolated second kit. Use the bundle when you want the shared base, Forge when you want Skyrim authoring, and this kit when you want the RE bench — any subset.

## Encyclopedia and Context7

`context7.json` at the repository root points [Context7](https://context7.com) at `encyclopedia/`: engine fingerprint cards, coordinate and depth conventions, IPC/shader blueprints, Ghidra/Frida/AOB recipes, version-pinned game notes, and an error registry. The encyclopedia ships at the kit root in the release and is indexed by Context7; the installer does not copy it into `sandbox/`. The 6-tier route ladder and the downstream-sink rule live in `encyclopedia/ROUTING.md`; every limit is scoped to a tool, version and escape hatch in `encyclopedia/CLAIMS.md`; anti-cheat risk tiers are in `encyclopedia/anticheat-risk.md`.

This is the tool bench. It works alone and composes with:

- [universal-modder](https://github.com/rehan-remade/universal-modder), for game reconnaissance, engine routes, builds, runtime checks and field notes
- [Ultimate AI Starter Bundle](https://github.com/SenjuWoo/Ultimate-AI-Starter-Bundle), for provider setup, shared compilers/runtimes, skills, scoped MCPs and task routing
- [Skyrim Forge](https://github.com/SenjuWoo/SkyrimForge) and other game forges, for game-specific authoring and specialist toolchains

The current Starter Bundle already provides `um` and the shared `universal-modder` skill. Reuse that installation. Reverse THIS! supplies the analysis tools; it does not install another fal MCP, rewrite provider configuration or grant project trust. Paid fal generation is optional and separate from local analysis.

universal-modder uses "mod hub" for a future place to publish mods. This repository is not that. It is the kit those agents install from.

## Install

Windows x64, PowerShell 5.1+. The installer targets Windows 10/11; individual tools and optional OS features have their own requirements. Double-click `install.cmd`, or from this folder:

```powershell
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\Install-Modkit.ps1
```

Normal runs resolve current owned release, pip, npm and NuGet versions, preserving intentional pins and numerically newer valid installations. Capa's package, rules and signatures remain one matched transaction. `-NoUpdate` retains valid installed versions; `-Offline` and `-Doctor` inspect files without downloads or writes; `-Plan` prints the catalog. Another checkout reuses the registered owner kit. Use `-InstallRoot` only for an explicit isolated installation; it never moves the shared pointer. See [standalone installation and updates](docs/standalone.md).

This repository installs independently. It reuses a validated shared base Python (`UABS_PYTHON` Process/User, then supported discovery) and compatible shared Node/npm; missing prerequisites use the official Python manager/winget packages. Manager-owned Python receives patch updates on its existing feature line; known winget-owned Node LTS receives targeted updates. App runtimes and venvs stay app-owned. Frida, capa, LIEF, FLOSS and UnityPy use separate dependency venvs; capa requires Python 3.10+, LIEF 3.9+, FLOSS 3.10+ and UnityPy 3.8+. ASAR needs Node 22.12+; REA has the stricter range in the [standalone guide](docs/standalone.md). Java routes share JDK 25 and ilspycmd uses .NET 10, provisioned through their official winget packages when missing. Runtime setup can change the machine; the bundle is optional.

Check the installed records and required files without downloading:

```powershell
.\Install-Modkit.ps1 -Doctor
```

The doctor checks files and records. Downloads are digest-verified when installed, and the install receipt binds each tool's shimmed executables and key files by SHA-256. `-Doctor` re-hashes those files and external commands it reuses: a changed one is reported `CHANGED` (exit 1; reinstall with `-Only <id> -Force`), a tool with no receipt is reported `UNVERIFIED`, and the rest of a payload is checked for presence and structure. Run the selected tool on a lab sample to verify its runtime and target support. Installer version, paths, progress and failures are in `sandbox/log/modkit.log`. Add `-DebugLog` to an install or repair run for detailed package/archive output; it is quiet by default.

Updates retain replaced tool payloads under `sandbox/backups/<id>-<unique-id>`. The previous installed manifest is saved as `sandbox/installed.json.pre-<kit-version>.json`. Failed updates restore the saved payload when one exists. Keep these backups until the replacement works on your target; they are local state, not release files.

## Using it

Double-click `install.cmd` once. After that, ask the AI; it resolves `%LOCALAPPDATA%\ReverseThis\kit.json`, reads `sandbox/installed.json`, and runs the `sandbox/bin` shims. You do not start individual tools or servers.

- Ghidra native analysis runs through `analyzeHeadless`; no server is required.
- Ghidra MCP is optional. `ghidra-mcp-up --launch` waits for both layers only when a bridge is configured; otherwise the AI uses `analyzeHeadless`.
- GUI tools are launched by the AI when the task needs them. If an agent asks you to run a start command, that is a kit bug, not a setup step.

## Isolated target work

The optional launcher uses Windows Sandbox when that feature is available on a supported Pro, Enterprise or Education edition. Windows Home users need a separate VM for isolation:

```powershell
.\Start-IsolatedLab.ps1 -Plan
.\Start-IsolatedLab.ps1 -InputFolder 'C:\Lab\Input'
```

The first command prints the configuration without launching or writing files. For the second, replace the example input path with an existing folder, or omit `-InputFolder`. It opens the guest with networking, clipboard, audio/video input, printer redirection and vGPU disabled. Tools and optional input are mapped read-only; the shared host output folder is `sandbox/work/isolated`. No target runs automatically. Host runtimes are not copied: Java, .NET, Python and Node routes can need a prepared VM or guest setup. Read-only tool folders can also prevent tools from saving settings beside their executables. The launcher does not enable Windows features or reboot the host.

## Where an agent looks

| Path | What it is |
|---|---|
| `sandbox/bin` | The only commands to call |
| `sandbox/tools/<id>` | The unpacked tool |
| `sandbox/installed.json` | Version, file, hash, shim for each tool |
| `sandbox/work` | Decomp, dumps and captures. Not part of the git repo |
| `sandbox/log/modkit.log` | Installer log: version, each download, each failure |
| `sandbox/backups` | Replaced tool payloads kept for rollback |
| `%LOCALAPPDATA%\ReverseThis\kit.json` | Machine pointer, for a chat that was not opened inside the clone |

Open the agent in this folder. It reads `AGENTS.md`. If the chat is somewhere else, the pointer file has the absolute kit root. Do not search `Program Files`, Downloads, or an old tools dump.

## What the click installs

Native: Ghidra (GUI and `analyzeHeadless`), ImHex, Detect It Easy (`diec`), x64dbg, PE-bear, RenderDoc (`renderdoccmd` and `qrenderdoc`), Rizin (`rizin`, `rz-bin`, `rz-diff`, `rz-asm`), System Informer, Dependencies (CLI and GUI), UPX, YARA (`yara` and `yarac`), GoReSym, FLOSS, MinHook, SafetyHook sources, Frida.

Local helpers: `pe-recon` prints a JSON PE fingerprint (bitness, graphics APIs, anti-cheat markers, RTTI, proxy candidates); `aob-gen` turns an RVA or byte pattern into a wildcard AOB signature; `ghidra-mcp-up` probes the Ghidra plugin (8080) and MCP bridge (8081). They are `local-script` catalog tools copied from `kit/`, and they do not execute the target.

Apps and formats: JADX (`jadx`, `jadx-gui`) for Android/Java bytecode; Vineflower for a second JVM decompile; Apktool for APK resource/smali decode and rebuild; `uber-apk-signer` for signing rebuilt APKs; `asar` for Electron archives; capa for static executable capability triage; Kaitai Struct compiler (`kaitai-struct-compiler`) for binary parsers; `wasm-tools` for WebAssembly; and `pyinstxtractor-ng` for extracting PyInstaller packages. Nintendo Switch: `nstool` for NSP/XCI/NCA/RomFS extraction and `switch-toolbox` for BNTX and common formats; the Pokémon-specific conversion route is in [docs/pokemon-switch.md](docs/pokemon-switch.md). League of Legends: `wadtools` lists and extracts `.wad.client` archives; the client-mod/LTK route is in [docs/league.md](docs/league.md). Naruto: the CPK/XFBIN and UE4 routes are in [docs/naruto-storm.md](docs/naruto-storm.md). capa uses the official `flare-capa` Python package with released rules in `sandbox/tools/capa-rules` and library-identification signatures in `sandbox/tools/capa-source/sigs`. Both dependencies install automatically, including for `-Only capa`; its shim supplies both paths. LIEF is a Python library in `sandbox/tools/lief`; use its `Scripts/python.exe` to import `lief`. UnityPy is the same pattern for Unity assets under `sandbox/tools/unitypy`. Extraction and capability matches need further analysis; they are not reconstructed source or proof of behavior.

.NET and Unity: ilspycmd, ILSpy, dnSpyEx, AssetRipper, Il2CppDumper, UABEA, BepInEx 5, MelonLoader, and the matching UnityExplorer plugins. UABEANext is the active Unity fork with dock support and nightly builds; see [docs/coverage.md](docs/coverage.md) for its manual/nightly route.

Unreal: FModel, repak, retoc, UAssetGUI, and the UE4SS experimental release (the stable tag is older; it is not what gets installed).

Source: Crowbar for models, BSPSource (`bspsrc`) for BSP maps, and Source 2 Viewer in both GUI (`source2viewer`) and CLI (`source2viewer-cli`) form.

REDengine: WolvenKit (`wolvenkit`) and its CLI (`wolvenkit-cli`) for Cyberpunk 2077 and The Witcher 3.

FromSoftware: Mod Engine 2 (`modengine2`) to load mods, and Smithbox to edit params, maps and regulation files.

GameMaker: UndertaleModTool (`undertalemodtool`) and its CLI (`utmt-cli`) for `data.win` files.

Total War: RPFM (`rpfm`) for pack files.

Frostbite: Frosty Editor and Frosty Mod Manager.

Godot: the current stable mono editor (`godot`, console build) and GDRE Tools for packed games.

Fighting games and pixel art: IKEMEN Go, Pixelorama, OpenBOR, and Fighter Factory (official Windows portable, x64). LDtk is unpacked from its Windows installer with 7-Zip. The installer is not run. `7z` is on `sandbox/bin`.

Helldivers 2: HD2Runtime SDK, runtime, example projects, and mod template; HD2Runtime ModBuilder; the community SDK; and Bingus Shared Loader.

Risk of Rain 2: R2API source from the current release tag.

Sleeping Dogs: TheoryEngine source, the SDK source, and the SDK example playground. The game executable and its PDB are not part of the kit.

Textures: `texconv`, `texassemble`, `texdiag`.

Drop-in loaders and SDKs stay in `sandbox/tools/<id>`. Copy a loader into a game folder when a mod needs it.

See [coverage and optional routes](docs/coverage.md) for Java editing, process monitoring, network inspection, SDKs and other gaps. Optional routes are not silently installed. A catalog entry or a successful installer is not proof that a particular app can be modified or rebuilt.

## What it refuses

Archived or replaced tools, stale binaries, website installers that bundle offers, paid shader packs, leaked private SDKs, game executables, and vendor GPU suites that install themselves into their own directories. An official rar or installer that 7-Zip can unpack into `sandbox/tools` is installed. The reasons are in [docs/currency.md](docs/currency.md). A portable tool you still want on the path, and that this kit will not download, goes in `sandbox/manual/<name>/`. The installer does not scan the rest of the disk.

## Rules

Do not commit game files, decompiled output, or the unpacked tools. Those stay under `sandbox/`. The kit does not download game executables or private symbol files. Whether a mod can be published is your call.

Each upstream tool keeps its own license. This repository is MIT.

## Development

Run these checks from a source checkout:

```powershell
python tests/test_catalog.py
python tests/test_encyclopedia.py
python tests/test_kit_tools.py
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\Install-Modkit.ps1 -SelfTest
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\Install-Modkit.ps1 -Plan
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\tests\test_installer.ps1
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\tests\test_isolation.ps1
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\tests\test_entrypoint.ps1
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\tests\test_dotnet_prerequisite.ps1
```

After installing the app/format tools, run `powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\tests\test_tool_smoke.ps1` from a source checkout for generated-sample checks, including APK resource modification/rebuilding and a static capa scan. Results/logs stay under `sandbox/work/runtime-smoke`. APK signing/device installation, graphical tools and game compatibility still need a real target test.

## Companion projects

Who owns what across the three repositories is stated once, in the [bundle README](https://github.com/SenjuWoo/Ultimate-AI-Starter-Bundle#ownership); the bundle installs this kit's latest release. Use the [Starter Bundle](https://github.com/SenjuWoo/Ultimate-AI-Starter-Bundle) for shared compilers, AI providers, skills and MCP routing. Use [Skyrim Forge](https://github.com/SenjuWoo/SkyrimForge) for Skyrim-specific authoring and validation. All three can coexist; this kit never rewrites provider configuration.

REA is selectable with `-Only rea` and included in the full catalog run. Its owner pin uses npm integrity with lifecycle scripts disabled. A matching or newer compatible same-major global REA is reused as unowned; unknown/older unowned copies are preserved and reported for their owner's update instead of duplicated. `-Force` explicitly installs an isolated owned copy. No `rea setup` or `rea update` is run. See [analysis routing](docs/reverse-engineering.md), [companion and snapshot routes](docs/companions.md), and [standalone installation/updates](docs/standalone.md).

ZIP installations update their public source through `Update-ReverseThis.ps1`, which requires the official release asset digest and retains source backups without changing private tools/work/state. Git development checkouts use their reviewed Git workflow. The updater's `-Plan`, `-Offline` and `-NoUpdate` modes write and download nothing.

## Product delivery

The versioned product ZIP contains runnable installers, catalog, usage instructions and required licensing. Source ledgers, changelogs, tests, workflows and private target work stay outside it. Maintainers build from an exact tested commit with `python scripts/build_release.py <new-output.zip> --commit <SHA>`; GitHub source downloads remain separately labelled source exports. Source upgrades retire only unchanged obsolete owned files into rollback backups.

```

`SECURITY.md`:

```md
# Security

Report a vulnerability in this repository through GitHub private vulnerability reporting on the repository.

https://github.com/SenjuWoo/reverse-this/security

Do not open a public issue for a security report.

The installer resolves official GitHub releases/source commits, RenderDoc stable archives and the official Fighter Factory portable page. HTTPS is required for downloads and redirects. GitHub assets use their exact SHA-256 digest or matching filename checksum when available; otherwise the manifest labels the locally computed hash. A locally computed hash records bytes but is not publisher authentication. NuGet, PyPI and npm tools keep their own registry trust model; npm lifecycle scripts are disabled. Versions are resolved at install time, so this is not an offline or reproducible dependency lock.

Required executables are checked before replacing a tool, with prior payloads retained under `sandbox/backups`. JSON state is replaced atomically; one installer holds an exclusive per-kit lock. This protects installation state, not the behavior of downloaded tools or analyzed targets.

The `sandbox` folder is not isolation. `Start-IsolatedLab.ps1` creates an optional Windows Sandbox configuration with networking and clipboard disabled, read-only tools/inputs and only `sandbox/work/isolated` writable from the guest. Guest output persists on the host: inspect it before opening or executing it. Guest runtimes and target compatibility need separate verification. Do not publish private dumps, proprietary targets or reconstructed code without appropriate rights.

```

- Companions: Starter Bundle owns general AI/build tools; Skyrim Forge owns Skyrim tooling/SDK guidance. Both are optional and mutually recommended.
- Run: install.cmd; read-only checks: Install-Modkit.ps1 -Doctor/-Offline. Update-ReverseThis.ps1 updates verified public source; Install-Modkit.ps1 refreshes the existing selection.
- Logs: <kit>/sandbox/log/modkit.log and source-update.log. Private tools, targets, state and backups remain outside Git/releases. Actual game/app GUI targets, paid APIs and guest sessions are not certified by source gates.

```

`Start-IsolatedLab.ps1`:

```ps1
#Requires -Version 5.1
<#
.SYNOPSIS
  Open the kit in Windows Sandbox with host tools and optional inputs read-only.
.DESCRIPTION
  -Plan prints the configuration without creating files or launching anything.
  Only sandbox/work/isolated is writable from the guest. No sample runs at logon.
#>
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [switch]$Plan,
    [string]$InputFolder
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$sandbox = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'sandbox'
$output = Join-Path $sandbox 'work\isolated'
$config = Join-Path $sandbox 'work\isolated-lab.wsb'
$mappings = @(
    @{ Host = (Join-Path $sandbox 'tools'); Guest = 'C:\ReverseThis\sandbox\tools'; ReadOnly = 'true' },
    @{ Host = (Join-Path $sandbox 'bin'); Guest = 'C:\ReverseThis\sandbox\bin'; ReadOnly = 'true' },
    @{ Host = $output; Guest = 'C:\ReverseThis\sandbox\work\isolated'; ReadOnly = 'false' }
)
if ($InputFolder) {
    $inputItem = Get-Item -LiteralPath $InputFolder
    if (-not $inputItem.PSIsContainer) { throw 'InputFolder must be a directory.' }
    $mappings += @{ Host = $inputItem.FullName; Guest = 'C:\ReverseThis\input'; ReadOnly = 'true' }
}

$settings = New-Object System.Xml.XmlWriterSettings
$settings.Indent = $true
$settings.OmitXmlDeclaration = $true
$text = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder
$writer = [System.Xml.XmlWriter]::Create($text, $settings)
try {
    $writer.WriteStartElement('Configuration')
    foreach ($name in @('Networking', 'ClipboardRedirection', 'AudioInput', 'VideoInput', 'PrinterRedirection', 'vGPU')) {
        $writer.WriteElementString($name, 'Disable')
    }
    $writer.WriteElementString('ProtectedClient', 'Enable')
    $writer.WriteStartElement('MappedFolders')
    foreach ($mapping in $mappings) {
        $writer.WriteStartElement('MappedFolder')
        $writer.WriteElementString('HostFolder', $mapping.Host)
        $writer.WriteElementString('SandboxFolder', $mapping.Guest)
        $writer.WriteElementString('ReadOnly', $mapping.ReadOnly)
        $writer.WriteEndElement()
    }
    $writer.WriteEndElement()
    $writer.WriteEndElement()
} finally {
    $writer.Dispose()
}
$xml = $text.ToString()
if ($Plan) { return $xml }

$launcher = Join-Path $env:WINDIR 'System32\WindowsSandbox.exe'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $launcher -PathType Leaf)) {
    throw 'Windows Sandbox is unavailable. It requires a supported Windows edition (Pro, Enterprise or Education) and the Windows Sandbox feature. -Plan works without it.'
}
foreach ($mapping in @($mappings | Where-Object { $_.ReadOnly -eq 'true' })) {
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $mapping.Host -PathType Container)) {
        throw "Missing mapped folder: $($mapping.Host). Install the kit first."
    }
}
# A writable junction must not redirect guest output outside the kit's work folder.
foreach ($folder in @($sandbox, (Join-Path $sandbox 'work'), $output, $config)) {
    if ((Test-Path -LiteralPath $folder) -and ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $folder -Force).Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint)) {
        throw "Writable output path contains a link: $folder"
    }
}
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $output | Out-Null
[IO.File]::WriteAllText($config, $xml, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding $false))
Write-Host "Guest output persists in: $output"
Start-Process -FilePath $launcher -ArgumentList ('"{0}"' -f $config)

```

`Update-ReverseThis.ps1`:

```ps1
#Requires -Version 5.1
<# Install or update a released workbench's public source from its official digest-verified archive.
   Private sandbox files/settings stay in place; retained backups and errors live under sandbox/backups and sandbox/log.
   Git checkouts use their normal reviewed Git workflow instead. Plan/Offline/NoUpdate write and download nothing. #>
[CmdletBinding()]
param([switch]$Plan, [switch]$Offline, [switch]$NoUpdate, [switch]$DebugLog, [string]$Archive, [string]$Sha256,
    [Alias('KitRoot')][string]$Destination = $PSScriptRoot)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

# Failures and version transitions remain local; detailed paths are opt-in.
function Write-SourceUpdateLog {
    param([string]$Message, [switch]$Detail)
    if (-not $sourceLog -or ($Detail -and -not $DebugLog)) { return }
    Add-Content -LiteralPath $sourceLog -Value ((Get-Date -Format 's') + ' ' + $Message) -Encoding UTF8
}

# Only repository source paths are eligible; release manifests never authorize private or Git writes.
function Test-PublicSourcePath {
    param([string]$Path)
    return $Path -match '^(?:[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9.-]*\.(?:ps1|cmd|md|txt|json)|VERSION|LICENSE|\.(?:gitattributes|gitignore)|(?:catalog|docs|tests|kit|encyclopedia|\.github)/[^:]+)$' -and
        $Path -notmatch '(^|/)\.\.(/|$)|\\|(^|/)\.git(/|$)'
}

# Existing links anywhere above a write could redirect an otherwise valid source path.
function Assert-SourceWritePath {
    param([string]$Path)
    $probe = $Path
    while ($probe) {
        if ((Test-Path -LiteralPath $probe) -and ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $probe -Force).Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint)) { throw "Linked source destination refused: $Path" }
        $parent = Split-Path -Parent $probe
        if ($parent -eq $probe) { break }
        $probe = $parent
    }
}

# Check paths, symlinks, file hashes and version before any installed source file is touched.
function Read-SourceRelease {
    param([string]$Directory)
    $manifest = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $Directory 'RELEASE.json') -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    if ($manifest.product -ne 'Reverse THIS!' -or $manifest.version -notmatch '^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$' -or
        $manifest.commit -notmatch '^[a-f0-9]{40}$' -or -not $manifest.files) { throw 'Invalid source release receipt.' }
    $seen = @{}
    foreach ($file in $manifest.files) {
        # Old release receipts include three harmless sandbox documentation placeholders; never deploy them.
        if ($file.path -in @('sandbox/README.md','sandbox/manual/README.md','sandbox/work/.gitkeep')) { continue }
        if (-not (Test-PublicSourcePath $file.path) -or $seen.ContainsKey($file.path) -or $file.sha256 -notmatch '^[a-f0-9]{64}$') { throw "Unsafe/duplicate source receipt path: $($file.path)" }
        $seen[$file.path] = $true
        $path = Join-Path $Directory $file.path
        Assert-SourceWritePath $path
        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $path -PathType Leaf) -or (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $path -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ne $file.sha256) { throw "Source receipt mismatch: $($file.path)" }
    }
    foreach ($required in @('Install-Modkit.ps1','VERSION','catalog/tools.json')) {
        if (-not $seen.ContainsKey($required)) { throw "Source receipt omitted $required" }
    }
    if ((Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $Directory 'VERSION') -Raw).Trim() -ne $manifest.version) { throw 'Source VERSION disagrees with its receipt.' }
    return $manifest
}

if ($Plan -or $Offline -or $NoUpdate) {
    Write-Host "Source update disabled/read-only. Owner: $Destination; official release: https://github.com/SenjuWoo/reverse-this/releases/latest"
    exit 0
}
$updateRoot = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Destination).TrimEnd('\')
if (-not [IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($Destination) -or $updateRoot -eq [IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($updateRoot).TrimEnd('\')) { throw 'Destination must be an absolute non-root directory.' }
$fresh = -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $updateRoot 'VERSION') -PathType Leaf)
if ($fresh -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $updateRoot) -and @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $updateRoot -Force).Count) { throw 'Nonempty unowned destination preserved; select an empty destination or an existing released workbench.' }
$currentVersion = if ($fresh) { '0.0.0' } else { (Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $updateRoot 'VERSION') -Raw).Trim() }
$ancestor = $updateRoot
while ($ancestor) {
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $ancestor '.git')) { throw 'Git development checkout preserved. Fetch/review and fast-forward through Git instead of the release updater.' }
    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $ancestor
    if ($parent -eq $ancestor) { break }
    $ancestor = $parent
}
Assert-SourceWritePath $updateRoot
$logDirectory = Join-Path $updateRoot 'sandbox\log'
Assert-SourceWritePath $logDirectory
$sourceLog = $null
if (-not $fresh) {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $logDirectory | Out-Null
    $sourceLog = Join-Path $logDirectory 'source-update.log'
    Write-SourceUpdateLog ('source update from ' + $currentVersion)
}
$temp = Join-Path ([IO.Path]::GetTempPath()) ('reverse-this-update-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
$lock = $null
$changed = @()
$backup = $null
try {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $temp | Out-Null
    if (-not $Archive) {
        $release = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri 'https://api.github.com/repos/SenjuWoo/reverse-this/releases/latest' -Headers @{ 'User-Agent'='ReverseTHIS-Updater' } -UseBasicParsing
        if ($release.tag_name -notmatch '^v\d+\.\d+\.\d+$') { throw 'Invalid official release version.' }
        if ([version]$release.tag_name.TrimStart('v') -le [version]$currentVersion) { Write-Host 'Workbench source is current or newer.'; exit 0 }
        $assets = @($release.assets | Where-Object { $_.name -eq ('Reverse-This-' + $release.tag_name.TrimStart('v') + '.zip') })
        if ($assets.Count -ne 1 -or $assets[0].digest -notmatch '^sha256:([a-f0-9]{64})$') { throw 'Official source archive lacks a unique SHA-256 asset digest.' }
        $Sha256 = $Matches[1]
        $url = [string]$assets[0].browser_download_url
        if ($url -notmatch '^https://github\.com/SenjuWoo/reverse-this/releases/download/v\d+\.\d+\.\d+/Reverse-This-\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.zip$') { throw 'Unexpected official archive URL.' }
        $Archive = Join-Path $temp 'release.zip'
        Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $url -OutFile $Archive -UseBasicParsing
    }
    if ($Sha256 -notmatch '^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$' -or (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Archive -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ne $Sha256) { throw 'Source archive SHA-256 mismatch or missing expected digest.' }
    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem
    $zip = [IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead($Archive)
    $unpack = Join-Path $temp 'source'
    try {
        foreach ($entry in $zip.Entries) {
            $path = $entry.FullName.Replace('\','/')
            if ($path -match '(^|/)\.\.(/|$)|^/|:' -or (($entry.ExternalAttributes -shr 16) -band 0xF000) -eq 0xA000) { throw 'Unsafe source archive entry.' }
        }
        [IO.Compression.ZipFileExtensions]::ExtractToDirectory($zip, $unpack)
    } finally { $zip.Dispose() }
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $unpack 'RELEASE.json'))) {
        $children = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $unpack -Directory)
        if ($children.Count -ne 1) { throw 'Expected one source release root.' }
        $unpack = $children[0].FullName
    }
    $manifest = Read-SourceRelease $unpack
    if ([version]$manifest.version -le [version]$currentVersion) { Write-Host 'Workbench source is current or newer.'; exit 0 }
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $logDirectory | Out-Null
    $sourceLog = Join-Path $logDirectory 'source-update.log'
    Write-SourceUpdateLog ('source update from ' + $currentVersion)
    Write-SourceUpdateLog -Detail "destination=$updateRoot release=$($manifest.version) commit=$($manifest.commit) files=$(@($manifest.files).Count)"
    $sandbox = Join-Path $updateRoot 'sandbox'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $sandbox | Out-Null
    $lock = [IO.File]::Open((Join-Path $sandbox '.install.lock'), 'OpenOrCreate', 'ReadWrite', 'None')
    # Only unchanged release-owned files may be replaced; new release paths must not collide with personal files.
    $previousPaths = @()
    if (-not $fresh) {
        try { $previous = Read-SourceRelease $updateRoot }
        catch { throw "Installed source was changed or its release receipt is invalid; preserved: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
        if ([version]$manifest.version -le [version]$previous.version) { Write-Host 'Workbench source advanced during download; current or newer source preserved.'; exit 0 }
        $previousPaths = @($previous.files | ForEach-Object path)
    }
    foreach ($file in $manifest.files) {
        if ((Test-PublicSourcePath $file.path) -and $file.path -notin $previousPaths -and
            (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $updateRoot $file.path))) { throw "New release path collides with a personal file; preserved: $($file.path)" }
    }
    $backup = Join-Path $sandbox ('backups/source-' + $manifest.version + '-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
    Assert-SourceWritePath $backup
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $backup -Force | Out-Null
    $incomingPaths = @($manifest.files | ForEach-Object path)
    foreach ($relative in $previousPaths) {
        if ($relative -in $incomingPaths -or -not (Test-PublicSourcePath $relative)) { continue }
        $target = Join-Path $updateRoot $relative
        Assert-SourceWritePath $target
        $old = Join-Path $backup $relative
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Split-Path -Parent $old) | Out-Null
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $target -Destination $old
        $changed += [pscustomobject]@{ path=$target; backup=$old; existed=$true }
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $target -Force
    }
    $paths = @($manifest.files | Where-Object { Test-PublicSourcePath $_.path } | ForEach-Object path) + @('RELEASE.json')
    foreach ($relative in $paths) {
        $target = Join-Path $updateRoot $relative
        $directory = Split-Path -Parent $target
        Assert-SourceWritePath $target
        $old = Join-Path $backup $relative
        $existed = Test-Path -LiteralPath $target
        if ($existed) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Split-Path -Parent $old) | Out-Null; Copy-Item -LiteralPath $target -Destination $old }
        $changed += [pscustomobject]@{ path=$target; backup=$old; existed=$existed }
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $directory | Out-Null
        $pending = Join-Path $directory ('.reverse-update-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
        try { Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $unpack $relative) -Destination $pending; Move-Item -LiteralPath $pending -Destination $target -Force }
        finally { if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $pending) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $pending -Force } }
    }
    Write-Host "Updated source to $($manifest.version). Previous files retained: $backup. Run Install-Modkit.ps1 to update selected tools."
    Write-SourceUpdateLog "source updated to $($manifest.version); rollback=$backup"
} catch {
    $failure = $_
    $rollbackErrors = @()
    foreach ($entry in @($changed | Sort-Object { $_.path.Length } -Descending)) {
        try {
            if ($entry.existed) { Copy-Item -LiteralPath $entry.backup -Destination $entry.path -Force }
            elseif (Test-Path -LiteralPath $entry.path) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $entry.path -Force }
        } catch { $rollbackErrors += $_.Exception.Message }
    }
    Write-SourceUpdateLog "FAIL $($failure.Exception.Message)"
    if ($rollbackErrors.Count) { throw "Source update failed: $($failure.Exception.Message). Rollback incomplete: $($rollbackErrors -join '; ')" }
    throw $failure
} finally {
    if ($lock) { $lock.Dispose() }
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $temp) {
        $resolved = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($temp)
        if ((Split-Path -Parent $resolved).TrimEnd('\') -ne ([IO.Path]::GetTempPath()).TrimEnd('\') -or
            (Split-Path -Leaf $resolved) -notmatch '^reverse-this-update-[a-f0-9]{32}$' -or
            ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $resolved -Force).Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint)) { throw 'Unsafe source-update temporary cleanup refused.' }
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $resolved -Recurse -Force
    }
}

```

# Validation

Historical source version: 1.2.1. Audit baselines: v1.1.0 at 94d27b7 and verified public v1.2.0 at 38f71b5. Previous source, release and installed-state rollback copies are retained.

Unsigned APKs are not installed and scanned PE samples are not executed. The other six generated-input checks below remain covered.

The host currently has no WindowsSandbox.exe; an actual guest session is unverified. Java/.NET/Python/Node runtimes are not provisioned into the guest.
- Fresh installation of the eight added capabilities passes in an isolated test profile. ASAR pack/list/extract and Wasm roundtrips preserve bytes; JADX recovers our generated Java class; Kaitai compiles a generated schema; LIEF parses an official tool's PE; pyinstxtractor-ng reads its own bundle metadata; Apktool help/version succeeds. Capa scans its own Python launcher using matched released rules/signatures. Its packed standalone EXE was blocked by host security, so the kit uses the upstream-supported Python route.
- Original-kit migration completes all 57 entries without failures. Doctor passes and all eight new-tool smoke checks pass from that install. Native-output rows are normalized; source archives use recorded commits. Previous payloads and the original state snapshot are retained. No game files were modified.
- Release evidence is recorded in GitHub's exact-SHA checks/release and the local `sandbox/work/release-validation.json` receipt after publication. CI, alert checks and public byte verification must be confirmed there; the local tests above do not substitute for those gates.

Runtime limits: graphical tools, real app/game modifications, RenderDoc Vulkan layer registration and target rebuilds are unverified. The installer downloads upstream tools at run time; an installer archive does not contain every upstream binary or runtime.

```

`VERSION`:

```
1.11.0

```

`WORKSPACE_OWNERSHIP.md`:

```md
# Source ownership

This root owns Reverse THIS! source. VERSION and Git tags identify the active version; preserve the existing public remote, MIT licence and authorized release workflow. Installed tools and target samples are separate owners, not source files. sandbox/work and sandbox/tools remain private, ignored and absent from product archives. Build product archives with scripts/build_release.py from the exact tested commit.

New work stays local/private until publication is requested. "Put it on GitHub" means public and MIT for original work by default, subject to third-party rights and platform terms; preserve existing licences and explicit choices. Generic publication uses the appropriate destination (normally Nexus Mods for Skyrim). A mod needing no compilation does not get a duplicate GitHub repository by default; add source hosting when requested or needed for development.

```

`context7.json`:

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://context7.com/schema/context7.json",
  "projectTitle": "AI Game Modding & Reverse Engineering Encyclopedia",
  "description": "Deterministic engine fingerprints, passthrough shader math, IPC seqlocks, wildcard AOB signatures, and Ghidra/Frida reverse engineering workflows for AI coding agents.",
  "folders": ["encyclopedia"],
  "excludeFolders": ["sandbox", "bin", ".git", "tests", "node_modules", "**/temp"],
  "excludeFiles": ["CHANGELOG.md", "LICENSE"],
  "rules": [
    "Evaluate the 6-Tier Modding Route Escalation Ladder in encyclopedia/ROUTING.md (Tier 1 data overrides -> Tier 2 loader APIs -> Tier 3 managed patching -> Tier 4 native proxy DLL/IAT hook -> Tier 5 headless sim/decomp library -> Tier 6 concurrent shared-memory passthrough) and stop at the lowest viable tier.",
    "Installation is not execution: prove every hook, proxy DLL and shader injection with a Downstream Sink canary (Present(), a UI text sink, or a frame-counter log) before claiming success.",
    "Validate Tier 4-6 native hooks and passthrough shaders in the offline harness (fake D3D11 host + IPC link test) and back up saves before a live single-player run.",
    "Scope IAT hooks to the target executable module only, with narrow path/flag predicates; anti-cheat is a risk decision: check the game's current policy, keep online changes cosmetic and free of competitive advantage, record the account risk, and note that memory injection can trigger detection even when the visual result is harmless; preserve FILE_FLAG_NO_BUFFERING on CreateFileW.",
    "Treat every limit as scoped to the named tool, version and mechanism. If a newer tool or runtime removes it, verify and update the card instead of repeating the old limit; see encyclopedia/CLAIMS.md."
  ]
}
