`kit/README.md`:

```md
# kit/ — local, dependency-light workbench helpers

These scripts are installed as `local-script` catalog tools. The installer copies
the selected script into `sandbox/tools/<id>/` and writes a versionless shim in
`sandbox/bin/`. They are part of the product ZIP; `sandbox/` is not.

| Tool | Shim | Runtime | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pe_recon.py` | `pe-recon.cmd` | shared Python, stdlib only | JSON PE fingerprint: bitness, graphics APIs, anti-cheat markers, RTTI strings, proxy DLL candidates |
| `aob_gen.py` | `aob-gen.cmd` | `capstone` venv | Wildcard AOB signature from an RVA or byte pattern; masks relocatable bytes only |
| `ghidra_mcp_up.py` | `ghidra-mcp-up.cmd` | shared Python, stdlib only | Probes the Ghidra plugin (8080) and MCP bridge (8081); `--launch` waits only when `GHIDRA_MCP_CMD` or an optional start.cmd exists. MCP is optional; unattended Ghidra uses `analyzeHeadless`. |

## Install

```powershell
.\Install-Modkit.ps1 -Only pe-recon,aob-gen,ghidra-mcp-up
```

`aob-gen` pulls `capstone` automatically. The other two use the kit's shared
Python; the installer provisions it when missing.

## Examples

```powershell
pe-recon "C:\Games\Target\Target.exe"
aob-gen "C:\Games\Target\Target.exe" --rva 0x1A2B30 --instructions 24
ghidra-mcp-up
ghidra-mcp-up --launch   # waits for both layers when GHIDRA_MCP_CMD or an optional start.cmd exists
```

## Rules

- These helpers read files and print JSON; they do not execute the target.
- `aob-gen` returns a signature, not a patch. Validate uniqueness in two builds.
- `ghidra-mcp-up` probes; it does not pretend to start a server that is not configured. If no launcher exists, use `analyzeHeadless` and leave MCP off.

```

`kit/aob_gen.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""aob_gen.py — generate an ASLR-safe wildcard AOB signature.

Masks relocatable displacement/immediate bytes while preserving opcode and ModRM
shapes. Requires Capstone (the kit installs it as the `capstone` catalog tool).

Usage:
    python aob_gen.py <file.exe> --rva 0x1A2B30 [--instructions 24]
    python aob_gen.py <file.exe> --pattern "48 8B 05 ?? ?? ?? ??" [--instructions 24]
    python aob_gen.py <file.exe> --offset 0x1200

Output JSON: pattern, rva, instructions, wildcards, unique, matches.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import struct
import sys
from pathlib import Path

try:
    from capstone import CS_ARCH_X86, CS_GRP_CALL, CS_GRP_JUMP, CS_MODE_32, CS_MODE_64, Cs
    from capstone.x86 import X86_OP_IMM, X86_OP_MEM, X86_REG_RIP
except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover - reported to the caller
    Cs = None  # type: ignore[assignment]


def u16(b: bytes, o: int) -> int:
    return struct.unpack_from("<H", b, o)[0]


def u32(b: bytes, o: int) -> int:
    return struct.unpack_from("<I", b, o)[0]


def u64(b: bytes, o: int) -> int:
    return struct.unpack_from("<Q", b, o)[0]


class PE:
    def __init__(self, data: bytes):
        self.data = data
        if data[:2] != b"MZ":
            raise ValueError("not a PE: missing MZ")
        self.e_lfanew = u32(data, 0x3C)
        if data[self.e_lfanew : self.e_lfanew + 4] != b"PE\0\0":
            raise ValueError("not a PE: missing PE signature")
        coff = self.e_lfanew + 4
        self.machine = u16(data, coff)
        self.section_count = u16(data, coff + 2)
        self.opt_size = u16(data, coff + 16)
        opt = coff + 20
        self.magic = u16(data, opt)
        self.pe32_plus = self.magic == 0x20B
        self.image_base = u64(data, opt + 24) if self.pe32_plus else u32(data, opt + 28)
        self.sections = []
        sec = opt + self.opt_size
        for i in range(self.section_count):
            base = sec + i * 40
            self.sections.append(
                {
                    "name": data[base : base + 8].rstrip(b"\0").decode("ascii", "replace"),
                    "virtual_size": u32(data, base + 8),
                    "virtual_address": u32(data, base + 12),
                    "raw_size": u32(data, base + 16),
                    "raw_offset": u32(data, base + 20),
                }
            )

    def rva_to_offset(self, rva: int) -> int | None:
        for s in self.sections:
            start = s["virtual_address"]
            size = max(s["virtual_size"], s["raw_size"])
            if start <= rva < start + size:
                delta = rva - start
                if delta < s["raw_size"]:
                    return s["raw_offset"] + delta
        return None

    def offset_to_rva(self, offset: int) -> int | None:
        for s in self.sections:
            start = s["raw_offset"]
            if start <= offset < start + s["raw_size"]:
                return s["virtual_address"] + (offset - start)
        return None


def parse_pattern(text: str) -> tuple[re.Pattern[bytes], bytes]:
    tokens = text.replace(",", " ").split()
    if not tokens:
        raise ValueError("empty pattern")
    regex = bytearray()
    fixed = bytearray()
    for token in tokens:
        if token in ("??", "?", "**"):
            regex += b"."
            fixed += b"\x00"
        else:
            value = int(token, 16)
            if not 0 <= value <= 0xFF:
                raise ValueError(f"byte out of range: {token}")
            regex += re.escape(bytes([value]))
            fixed += bytes([value])
    return re.compile(bytes(regex), re.DOTALL), bytes(fixed)


def mask_flags(insn, length: int) -> bytearray:
    """Return a flag per byte: 1 = wildcard, 0 = keep.

    Only relocatable bytes are masked: RIP-relative disp32, branch/call
    displacements and absolute address immediates. Small constants stay.
    """
    flags = bytearray(length)
    branch = insn.group(CS_GRP_JUMP) or insn.group(CS_GRP_CALL)
    for op in insn.operands:
        if op.type == X86_OP_MEM and op.mem.base == X86_REG_RIP:
            for i in range(max(0, length - 4), length):
                flags[i] = 1
        elif op.type == X86_OP_IMM and op.size:
            if branch or op.size == 8 or (op.size == 4 and op.imm >= 0x10000):
                for i in range(max(0, length - op.size), length):
                    flags[i] = 1
    if branch:
        size = 1 if length <= 2 else 4
        for i in range(max(0, length - size), length):
            flags[i] = 1
    return flags


def emit(original: bytes | bytearray, masked: bytearray) -> str:
    parts = []
    for i, byte in enumerate(original):
        parts.append("??" if masked[i] else f"{byte:02X}")
    return " ".join(parts)


def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("file")
    source = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    source.add_argument("--rva", type=lambda s: int(s, 0))
    source.add_argument("--offset", type=lambda s: int(s, 0))
    source.add_argument("--pattern")
    parser.add_argument("--instructions", type=int, default=24)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv[1:])

    if Cs is None:
        print(json.dumps({"error": "capstone is not installed; run install.cmd -Only aob-gen"}))
        return 3

    path = Path(args.file)
    try:
        data = path.read_bytes()
        pe = PE(data)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - report the parse failure as JSON
        print(json.dumps({"file": str(path), "error": str(exc)}))
        return 2

    if args.pattern:
        regex, _ = parse_pattern(args.pattern)
        match = regex.search(data)
        if not match:
            print(json.dumps({"file": str(path), "error": "pattern not found"}))
            return 1
        offset = match.start()
    elif args.rva is not None:
        offset = pe.rva_to_offset(args.rva)
        if offset is None:
            print(json.dumps({"file": str(path), "error": "RVA is not in a raw section"}))
            return 1
    else:
        offset = args.offset
        if not (0 <= offset < len(data)):
            print(json.dumps({"file": str(path), "error": "offset out of range"}))
            return 1

    rva = pe.offset_to_rva(offset)
    if rva is None:
        print(json.dumps({"file": str(path), "error": "offset is not in a mapped section"}))
        return 1

    mode = CS_MODE_64 if pe.pe32_plus else CS_MODE_32
    md = Cs(CS_ARCH_X86, mode)
    md.detail = True
    va = pe.image_base + rva
    window = data[offset : offset + 4096]
    original = bytearray()
    wildcard_flags = bytearray()
    count = 0
    for insn in md.disasm(window, va):
        if count >= args.instructions:
            break
        raw = bytearray(insn.bytes)
        original += raw
        wildcard_flags += mask_flags(insn, len(raw))
        count += 1
        if len(original) >= len(window):
            break

    if count == 0:
        print(json.dumps({"file": str(path), "error": "no instructions decoded"}))
        return 1

    text = emit(original, wildcard_flags)
    tokens = text.split()
    regex = b"".join(b"." if t == "??" else re.escape(bytes([int(t, 16)])) for t in tokens)
    matches = len(re.findall(regex, data, re.DOTALL))
    result = {
        "file": str(path),
        "pattern": text,
        "rva": hex(rva),
        "offset": hex(offset),
        "instructions": count,
        "wildcards": sum(1 for t in tokens if t == "??"),
        "bytes": len(tokens),
        "unique": matches == 1,
        "matches": matches,
    }
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
    return 0 if matches == 1 else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv))

```

`kit/ghidra_mcp_up.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""ghidra_mcp_up.py — check or launch the optional local Ghidra MCP stack.

The stack has two layers:
  Ghidra plugin HTTP   127.0.0.1:8080  (GhidraMCP extension)
  MCP streamable HTTP  127.0.0.1:8081/mcp (bridge process)

Usage:
    python ghidra_mcp_up.py            # JSON status, exit 0 only if both answer
    python ghidra_mcp_up.py --text     # one line per layer
    python ghidra_mcp_up.py --launch   # start GHIDRA_MCP_CMD or an optional start.cmd,
                                       # wait for both layers, then print status

Unattended Ghidra work does not need this stack: use analyzeHeadless. MCP is an
optional interactive bridge; the agent starts it only when a launcher exists.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

GHIDRA_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8080/"
MCP_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8081/mcp"


def kit_root() -> Path:
    """Resolve the kit from env, then from this file, then from the cwd."""
    explicit = os.environ.get("REVERSE_THIS_KIT_ROOT")
    if explicit:
        return Path(explicit)
    here = Path(__file__).resolve()
    for parent in [here.parent, *here.parents]:
        if (parent / "sandbox").is_dir() and (parent / "catalog").is_dir():
            return parent
    return Path.cwd()


MANUAL_START = kit_root() / "sandbox/manual/ghidra-mcp/start.cmd"


def probe(url: str, timeout: float = 1.5) -> dict:
    request = urllib.request.Request(url, method="GET")
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
            return {"url": url, "up": True, "status": response.status}
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        # Any HTTP answer means the listener is alive (405/400 are common here).
        return {"url": url, "up": True, "status": exc.code}
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - connection errors are the normal miss
        return {"url": url, "up": False, "status": None, "error": str(exc)}


def snapshot() -> dict:
    ghidra = probe(GHIDRA_URL)
    mcp = probe(MCP_URL)
    return {
        "ghidra_plugin": ghidra,
        "mcp_bridge": mcp,
        "ready": ghidra["up"] and mcp["up"],
    }


def launch() -> int:
    status = snapshot()
    if status["ready"]:
        print(json.dumps(status, indent=2))
        return 0

    command = os.environ.get("GHIDRA_MCP_CMD")
    if not command and MANUAL_START.is_file():
        command = str(MANUAL_START)
    if not command:
        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "error": "no Ghidra MCP launcher configured",
                    "optional": True,
                    "fix": "Use analyzeHeadless for unattended Ghidra analysis. "
                    "Set GHIDRA_MCP_CMD only if a Ghidra MCP bridge is installed.",
                }
            )
        )
        return 2

    subprocess.Popen(command, shell=True)
    wait = int(os.environ.get("GHIDRA_MCP_WAIT", "60"))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0, wait)
    while True:
        status = snapshot()
        if status["ready"]:
            print(json.dumps({"launched": command, **status}, indent=2))
            return 0
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            print(json.dumps({"launched": command, **status}, indent=2))
            return 1
        time.sleep(2)


def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
    if "--launch" in argv:
        return launch()

    status = snapshot()
    if "--text" in argv:
        for name, row in (("ghidra", status["ghidra_plugin"]), ("mcp", status["mcp_bridge"])):
            state = "up" if row["up"] else "down"
            print(f"{name}: {state} {row['url']} status={row['status']}")
    else:
        print(json.dumps(status, indent=2))
    return 0 if status["ready"] else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv))

```

`kit/pe_recon.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""pe_recon.py — deterministic PE reconnaissance for the Reverse THIS! kit.

Outputs JSON: bitness, machine, subsystem, entry point, sections, imported DLLs,
graphics APIs, anti-cheat markers, RTTI strings and safe proxy DLL candidates.
Standard library only; no target execution.

Usage:
    python pe_recon.py <file.exe> [--json]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import struct
import sys
from pathlib import Path

GRAPHICS_DLLS = {
    "d3d9.dll": "Direct3D 9",
    "d3d10.dll": "Direct3D 10",
    "d3d11.dll": "Direct3D 11",
    "d3d12.dll": "Direct3D 12",
    "dxgi.dll": "DXGI",
    "opengl32.dll": "OpenGL",
    "vulkan-1.dll": "Vulkan",
    "ddraw.dll": "DirectDraw",
    "gdi32.dll": "GDI",
    "d2d1.dll": "Direct2D",
    "dwrite.dll": "DirectWrite",
}

ANTI_CHEAT_MARKERS = {
    "BattlEye": ["battleye", "beclient", "beservice"],
    "EasyAntiCheat": ["easyanticheat", "easyanti", "eac_launcher"],
    "Riot Vanguard": ["vgk.sys", "vgc.dll", "vanguard"],
    "Denuvo": ["denuvo"],
    "VMProtect": ["vmprotect"],
    "Themida": ["themida", "winlicense"],
    "Arxan": ["arxan"],
    "PunkBuster": ["punkbuster", "pnkbstr"],
    "Ricochet": ["ricochet"],
    "nProtect": ["nprotect", "gameguard"],
}

PROXY_CANDIDATES = [
    "dinput8.dll",
    "winmm.dll",
    "version.dll",
    "dxgi.dll",
    "d3d11.dll",
    "xinput1_4.dll",
    "xinput1_3.dll",
    "dwmapi.dll",
]


def u16(b: bytes, o: int) -> int:
    return struct.unpack_from("<H", b, o)[0]


def u32(b: bytes, o: int) -> int:
    return struct.unpack_from("<I", b, o)[0]


def u64(b: bytes, o: int) -> int:
    return struct.unpack_from("<Q", b, o)[0]


def cstr(b: bytes, o: int, limit: int = 512) -> str:
    end = b.find(b"\0", o, o + limit)
    if end < 0:
        end = min(o + limit, len(b))
    return b[o:end].decode("ascii", "replace")


class PE:
    def __init__(self, data: bytes):
        self.data = data
        if data[:2] != b"MZ":
            raise ValueError("not a PE: missing MZ")
        self.e_lfanew = u32(data, 0x3C)
        if data[self.e_lfanew : self.e_lfanew + 4] != b"PE\0\0":
            raise ValueError("not a PE: missing PE signature")
        coff = self.e_lfanew + 4
        self.machine = u16(data, coff)
        self.section_count = u16(data, coff + 2)
        self.timestamp = u32(data, coff + 4)
        self.opt_size = u16(data, coff + 16)
        self.characteristics = u16(data, coff + 18)
        opt = coff + 20
        self.opt = opt
        self.magic = u16(data, opt)
        self.pe32_plus = self.magic == 0x20B
        self.entry_rva = u32(data, opt + 16)
        self.image_base = u64(data, opt + 24) if self.pe32_plus else u32(data, opt + 28)
        self.subsystem = u16(data, opt + 68)
        self.dll_characteristics = u16(data, opt + 70)
        self.dir_count = u32(data, opt + (108 if self.pe32_plus else 92))
        self.dirs_at = opt + (112 if self.pe32_plus else 96)
        self.sections = []
        sec = opt + self.opt_size
        for i in range(self.section_count):
            base = sec + i * 40
            name = data[base : base + 8].rstrip(b"\0").decode("ascii", "replace")
            self.sections.append(
                {
                    "name": name,
                    "virtual_size": u32(data, base + 8),
                    "virtual_address": u32(data, base + 12),
                    "raw_size": u32(data, base + 16),
                    "raw_offset": u32(data, base + 20),
                    "characteristics": u32(data, base + 36),
                }
            )

    def dir(self, index: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
        if index >= self.dir_count:
            return (0, 0)
        return (u32(self.data, self.dirs_at + index * 8), u32(self.data, self.dirs_at + index * 8 + 4))

    def rva_to_offset(self, rva: int) -> int | None:
        for s in self.sections:
            start = s["virtual_address"]
            size = max(s["virtual_size"], s["raw_size"])
            if start <= rva < start + size:
                delta = rva - start
                if delta < s["raw_size"]:
                    return s["raw_offset"] + delta
        return None

    def imports(self) -> list[str]:
        rva, size = self.dir(1)
        if not rva:
            return []
        off = self.rva_to_offset(rva)
        if off is None:
            return []
        names = []
        for i in range(0, 4096, 20):
            base = off + i
            if base + 20 > len(self.data):
                break
            name_rva = u32(self.data, base + 12)
            if name_rva == 0:
                break
            name_off = self.rva_to_offset(name_rva)
            if name_off is not None:
                names.append(cstr(self.data, name_off))
        return sorted(set(n.lower() for n in names))

    def rtti_strings(self) -> list[str]:
        out: list[str] = []
        for s in self.sections:
            if s["name"] not in (".rdata", ".data"):
                continue
            blob = self.data[s["raw_offset"] : s["raw_offset"] + s["raw_size"]]
            for marker in (b".?AV", b".?AU"):
                start = 0
                while True:
                    idx = blob.find(marker, start)
                    if idx < 0:
                        break
                    text = cstr(blob, idx, 256)
                    if 4 <= len(text) <= 200 and text not in out:
                        out.append(text)
                    start = idx + 4
                    if len(out) >= 60:
                        return out
        return out


def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
    if len(argv) < 2 or argv[1] in ("-h", "--help"):
        print((__doc__ or "").strip())
        return 2
    path = Path(argv[1])
    try:
        data = path.read_bytes()
        pe = PE(data)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - report the parse failure as JSON
        print(json.dumps({"file": str(path), "error": str(exc)}, indent=2))
        return 2

    imports = pe.imports()
    graphics = [
        {"dll": dll, "api": GRAPHICS_DLLS[dll]}
        for dll in imports
        if dll in GRAPHICS_DLLS
    ]
    lowered = data.lower()
    anti_cheat = [
        name
        for name, needles in ANTI_CHEAT_MARKERS.items()
        if any(n.encode("ascii") in lowered for n in needles)
    ]
    proxy = [
        {"name": name, "already_imported": name in imports}
        for name in PROXY_CANDIDATES
    ]
    result = {
        "file": str(path),
        "size": len(data),
        "sha256_note": "hash the file separately; this tool does not hash large targets",
        "bitness": 64 if pe.pe32_plus else 32,
        "machine": hex(pe.machine),
        "subsystem": pe.subsystem,
        "entry_rva": hex(pe.entry_rva),
        "image_base": hex(pe.image_base),
        "dll": bool(pe.characteristics & 0x2000),
        "sections": pe.sections,
        "imports": imports,
        "graphics_apis": graphics,
        "anti_cheat_markers": anti_cheat,
        "rtti_strings": pe.rtti_strings(),
        "proxy_candidates": proxy,
    }
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv))

`Install-Modkit.ps1` fills `tools\` and `bin\`. Those directories are gitignored.

`work\` is for decomp, dumps and captures. One folder per game. Do not commit it.

`scripts/build_release.py`:

```py
"""Build the runnable workbench from a frozen Git commit, never a private working tree."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, hashlib, io, json, re, subprocess, zipfile
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PRODUCT_FILES = {'VERSION','LICENSE','README.md','AGENTS.md','CLAUDE.md','Install-Modkit.ps1',
                 'Update-ReverseThis.ps1','Start-IsolatedLab.ps1','install.cmd','catalog/asset-pattern-fixtures.json',
                 'context7.json','kit/README.md','kit/pe_recon.py','kit/aob_gen.py','kit/ghidra_mcp_up.py'}
# The encyclopedia ships with the kit so the local Context7 fallback works offline.
ENCYCLOPEDIA_REQUIRED = {'encyclopedia/README.md','encyclopedia/ROUTING.md','encyclopedia/ERROR-REGISTRY.json'}


def build(root: Path, output: Path, commit: str = 'HEAD') -> dict:
    sha = subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse',commit+'^{commit}'],cwd=root,text=True).strip()
    raw = subprocess.check_output(['git','archive','--format=zip',sha],cwd=root)
    files = {}
    with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(raw)) as source:
        for entry in source.infolist():
            name = entry.filename
            if entry.is_dir() or not (name in PRODUCT_FILES or name.startswith(('catalog/','docs/','encyclopedia/'))):
                continue
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or '..' in rel.parts or '\\' in name or ':' in name or ((entry.external_attr >> 16) & 0o170000) == 0o120000:
                raise ValueError('Unsafe product source: '+name)
            if 'changelog' in rel.stem.casefold() and rel.suffix.casefold() in {'.txt','.md','.rst'}:
                continue
            data = source.read(entry)
            if rel.suffix.casefold() in {'.ps1','.cmd','.bat'}:
                data = data.replace(b'\r\n',b'\n').replace(b'\n',b'\r\n')
            files[name] = data
    required = PRODUCT_FILES | ENCYCLOPEDIA_REQUIRED | {'catalog/tools.json'}
    if not required <= files.keys():
        raise ValueError('Missing product files: '+str(sorted(required-files.keys())))
    version = files['VERSION'].decode('utf-8-sig').strip()
    if not re.fullmatch(r'\d+\.\d+\.\d+',version):
        raise ValueError('Invalid product version')
    manifest = {'product':'Reverse THIS!','version':version,'commit':sha,'files':[
        {'path':name,'size':len(data),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()} for name,data in sorted(files.items())]}
    files['RELEASE.json'] = (json.dumps(manifest,indent=2)+'\n').encode()
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    with output.open('xb') as stream:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(stream,'w',zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,compresslevel=9) as archive:
            for name,data in sorted(files.items()):
                info=zipfile.ZipInfo(f'Reverse-This-{version}/'+name,(2026,10,27,0,0,0))
                info.create_system=3; info.external_attr=0o100644<<16
                archive.writestr(info,data,compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,compresslevel=9)
    with zipfile.ZipFile(output) as archive:
        if archive.testzip():
            raise ValueError('Product archive CRC failure')
    return {'version':version,'commit':sha,'files':len(files),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(output.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'bytes':output.stat().st_size}


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('output',type=Path);parser.add_argument('--commit',default='HEAD')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    print(json.dumps(build(ROOT,args.output,args.commit)))

```

`scripts/check_companions.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Read-only compatibility check against the published companion contracts.

Checks the Starter Bundle's reverse-this catalog entry (minimum version, asset
template, owner) and the Skyrim Forge ownership link. It never writes to either
repository. Exit 0 = compatible, 1 = drift, 2 = the published contract could not
be fetched.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BUNDLE_CATALOG = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/SenjuWoo/Ultimate-AI-Starter-Bundle/main/BUNDLED-TOOLS/CATALOG.json"
BUNDLE_README = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/SenjuWoo/Ultimate-AI-Starter-Bundle/main/README.md"
FORGE_README = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/SenjuWoo/SkyrimForge/main/README.md"


def fetch(url: str) -> str:
    request = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "reverse-this-companion-check"})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=20) as response:
        return response.read().decode("utf-8-sig")


def version_tuple(value: str) -> tuple[int, ...]:
    parts = [int(part) for part in value.strip().lstrip("v").split(".") if part.isdigit()]
    return tuple((parts + [0, 0, 0])[:3])


def main() -> int:
    version = (ROOT / "VERSION").read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").strip()
    errors: list[str] = []
    try:
        catalog = json.loads(fetch(BUNDLE_CATALOG))
        entries = [row for row in catalog.get("components", []) if row.get("id") == "reverse-this"]
        if len(entries) != 1:
            errors.append("bundle catalog must declare exactly one reverse-this component")
        else:
            entry = entries[0]
            floor = str(entry.get("min_version", ""))
            if version_tuple(floor) > version_tuple(version):
                errors.append(f"bundle floor {floor} is newer than this kit {version}")
            if entry.get("asset_template") != "Reverse-This-{version}.zip":
                errors.append(f"bundle asset template changed: {entry.get('asset_template')}")
            github = entry.get("github", {})
            if (github.get("owner"), github.get("repo")) != ("SenjuWoo", "reverse-this"):
                errors.append("bundle catalog points at a different repository")

        bundle_readme = fetch(BUNDLE_README)
        if "Reverse THIS!" not in bundle_readme:
            errors.append("bundle README no longer names Reverse THIS!")
        if "skyrim,reverse" not in bundle_readme:
            errors.append("bundle README no longer documents the reverse pack selection")

        forge_readme = fetch(FORGE_README)
        if "Reverse THIS!" not in forge_readme:
            errors.append("Forge README no longer names Reverse THIS!")
        if "bundle README" not in forge_readme:
            errors.append("Forge README no longer links the bundle ownership section")
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a fetch failure is unknown, not drift
        print("UNKNOWN: companion contract could not be fetched: " + str(exc))
        return 2

    if errors:
        for error in errors:
            print("DRIFT: " + error)
        return 1
    print(f"companions ok: reverse-this {version}, bundle floor compatible, asset template and Forge ownership link present")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

```

`tests/test_catalog.py`:

```py
"""Catalog contract. Stdlib only. Run: python tests/test_catalog.py"""

import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
METHODS = {
    "github-release",
    "github-files",
    "renderdoc-stable-zip",
    "dotnet-tool",
    "pip-venv",
    "npm-tool",
    "github-archive",
    "virtualltek-portable",
    "local-script",
}
UNPACK = {None, "7z", "msi-admin", "nsis-app64", "raw"}


def load(name):
    return json.loads((ROOT / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def one_match(names, pattern):
    hits = [name for name in names if re.search(pattern, name)]
    return hits


def renderdoc_zip_url(tag):
    if not re.fullmatch(r"v\d+\.\d+", tag):
        raise ValueError(tag)
    version = tag[1:]
    return "https://renderdoc.org/stable/{0}/RenderDoc_{0}_64.zip".format(version)


def release_api(repo, release):
    if release:
        return "https://api.github.com/repos/{0}/releases/tags/{1}".format(repo, release)
    return "https://api.github.com/repos/{0}/releases/latest".format(repo)


def main():
    errors = []
    catalog = load("catalog/tools.json")
    fixture = load("catalog/asset-pattern-fixtures.json")
    version = (ROOT / "VERSION").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    changelog = (ROOT / "CHANGELOG.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    readme = (ROOT / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    agents = (ROOT / "AGENTS.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    currency = (ROOT / "docs/currency.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    if not re.fullmatch(r"\d+\.\d+\.\d+", version):
        errors.append("VERSION is not semver")
    if "## {0}".format(version) not in changelog:
        errors.append("CHANGELOG.md is missing heading {0}".format(version))

    for needle in (
        "https://github.com/rehan-remade/universal-modder",
        "https://github.com/SenjuWoo/Ultimate-AI-Starter-Bundle",
        "Install-Modkit.ps1",
        "sandbox/bin",
        "sandbox/installed.json",
        "%LOCALAPPDATA%\\ReverseThis\\kit.json",
    ):
        if needle not in readme or needle not in agents:
            errors.append("missing contract text in README or AGENTS: {0}".format(needle))
    for needle in ("## Modular composition", "UABS_PYTHON", "Skyrim Forge", "no Skyrim tools"):
        if needle not in readme:
            errors.append("README is missing modular composition text: {0}".format(needle))

    ids = []
    for tool in catalog["tools"]:
        tid = tool["id"]
        ids.append(tid)
        if tid != tid.lower() or not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z0-9-]+", tid):
            errors.append("bad id {0}".format(tid))
        if tool["method"] not in METHODS:
            errors.append("{0} has unknown method".format(tid))
        if tool["method"] == "github-release":
            if tid not in fixture:
                errors.append("{0} has no asset fixture".format(tid))
            else:
                hits = one_match(fixture[tid]["assets"], tool["asset_pattern"])
                if hits != [fixture[tid]["expect"]]:
                    errors.append("{0} pattern matched {1}".format(tid, hits))
        if tool["method"] == "github-files":
            missing = [name for name in tool["files"] if name not in fixture[tid]["assets"]]
            if missing:
                errors.append("{0} files missing from fixture {1}".format(tid, missing))
            if any(name.endswith("_arm64.exe") for name in tool["files"]):
                errors.append("{0} selected an arm64 exe".format(tid))
        if tool["method"] in ("dotnet-tool", "pip-venv", "npm-tool") and not tool.get("package"):
            errors.append("{0} needs a package".format(tid))
        if "repo" in tool and tool["repo"].count("/") != 1:
            errors.append("{0} repo is not owner/name".format(tid))
        if tool.get("unpack") not in UNPACK:
            errors.append("{0} has unknown unpack".format(tid))
        if tool.get("unpack") == "msi-admin" and tid != "7zip":
            errors.append("{0} is not allowed to extract an MSI".format(tid))
        if tool.get("unpack") == "nsis-app64" and tid != "ldtk":
            errors.append("{0} is not allowed to unpack an app-64 installer".format(tid))
        if tool["method"] == "github-archive" and tool.get("ref") not in (None, "release"):
            errors.append("{0} archive ref must be release or omitted".format(tid))
        if tool["method"] == "virtualltek-portable" and tid != "fighter-factory":
            errors.append("{0} is not the Fighter Factory portable".format(tid))

    if len(ids) != len(set(ids)):
        errors.append("duplicate tool ids")

    skyrim = [tool["id"] for tool in catalog["tools"]
              if any(k in (tool["id"] + " " + tool["name"]).lower()
                     for k in ("skyrim", "xedit", "sseedit", "loot", "housecarl", "papyrus", "creation kit"))]
    if skyrim:
        errors.append("catalog must not install Forge-owned Skyrim tools: {0}".format(skyrim))

    installable = set(ids)
    for tool in catalog["tools"]:
        if tool["method"] == "local-script":
            script = tool.get("script")
            if not script or not (ROOT / script).is_file():
                errors.append("{0} has no local script".format(tool["id"]))
            python = tool.get("python")
            if python not in (["shared"] + ids):
                errors.append("{0} has unknown python {1}".format(tool["id"], python))
            for shim in tool.get("shims", []):
                if not shim.get("python_script"):
                    errors.append("{0} shim is not a python script".format(tool["id"]))
        for dependency in tool.get("requires", []):
            if dependency not in installable or dependency == tool["id"]:
                errors.append("{0} has invalid dependency {1}".format(tool["id"], dependency))
    for row in catalog["retired"]:
        if row["id"] in installable:
            errors.append("retired id is also installed: {0}".format(row["id"]))
        if row["id"] not in currency:
            errors.append("currency.md does not mention {0}".format(row["id"]))
        instead = row.get("instead")
        if instead is not None and instead not in installable:
            errors.append("retired {0} points at unknown {1}".format(row["id"], instead))

    if renderdoc_zip_url("v1.46") != "https://renderdoc.org/stable/1.46/RenderDoc_1.46_64.zip":
        errors.append("renderdoc url builder drifted")
    try:
        renderdoc_zip_url("nightly")
        errors.append("renderdoc url builder accepted a bad tag")
    except ValueError:
        pass

    ue = next(tool for tool in catalog["tools"] if tool["id"] == "ue4ss")
    if release_api(ue["repo"], ue["release"]).endswith("/releases/latest"):
        errors.append("ue4ss must not track the stable latest release")

    ff_download = (ROOT / "catalog/selftest/fighter-factory-download.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    ff_topic = (ROOT / "catalog/selftest/fighter-factory-topic.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    ff_expect = "https://virtualltek.com/wp-content/uploads/wpforo/default_attachments/1/1707418391-375_windows_port.zip"
    ff_topic_match = re.search(r'href="(/community/[^"]*winport[^"]*)"', ff_download)
    if ff_topic_match is None or not re.fullmatch(r"/community/[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9./_-]*winport/?", ff_topic_match.group(1)):
        errors.append("fighter factory download fixture did not resolve a portable topic")
    elif ff_download.find("ff-375-win\"") > ff_download.find("ff-375-winport"):
        errors.append("fighter factory fixture no longer puts the installer topic first")
    ff_urls = list(dict.fromkeys(re.findall(r"https://virtualltek\.com/wp-content/uploads/[A-Za-z0-9_./-]+\.zip", ff_topic)))
    if ff_urls != [ff_expect]:
        errors.append("fighter factory topic fixture resolved {0}".format(ff_urls))

    if errors:
        print("\n".join(errors))
        return 1
    print("catalog ok: {0} tools, version {1}".format(len(ids), version))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`tests/test_delivery.py`:

```py
"""Prove product selection excludes private working state, with actual local Git objects."""
import hashlib, importlib.util, json, os, subprocess, tempfile, unittest, zipfile
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('delivery',ROOT/'scripts/build_release.py')
builder=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(builder)

class ProductTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @unittest.skipUnless(os.name=='nt','Windows product entrypoints')
    def test_actual_extracted_selftest_and_plan(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='Reverse product ! ') as td:
            base=Path(td); archive=base/'product.zip';builder.build(ROOT,archive)
            with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as z:z.extractall(base)
            version=(ROOT/'VERSION').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig').strip()
            product=base/f'Reverse-This-{version}'
            for switch in ('-SelfTest','-Plan'):
                result=subprocess.run(['powershell.exe','-NoProfile','-ExecutionPolicy','Bypass','-File',str(product/'Install-Modkit.ps1'),switch],capture_output=True,text=True,
                                      encoding='utf-8',errors='replace',timeout=30)
                self.assertEqual(result.returncode,0,result.stdout+result.stderr)
            self.assertFalse((product/'sandbox').exists(),'Read-only entrypoints wrote product state')
            # The bundle calls this updater; it must accept the encyclopedia and kit paths too.
            digest=hashlib.sha256(archive.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            deployed=base/'deployed'
            result=subprocess.run(['powershell.exe','-NoProfile','-ExecutionPolicy','Bypass','-File',str(ROOT/'Update-ReverseThis.ps1'),
                                   '-Destination',str(deployed),'-Archive',str(archive),'-Sha256',digest],capture_output=True,text=True,
                                  encoding='utf-8',errors='replace',timeout=120)
            self.assertEqual(result.returncode,0,result.stdout+result.stderr)
            self.assertEqual((deployed/'VERSION').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig').strip(),version)
            self.assertTrue((deployed/'context7.json').is_file())
            self.assertTrue((deployed/'encyclopedia/README.md').is_file())
            self.assertTrue((deployed/'kit/README.md').is_file())

    def test_frozen_product_reproducible_private_files_absent_and_existing_output_preserved(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='Reverse ! ') as td:
            root=Path(td)
            def git(*args):
                return subprocess.run(['git','-C',str(root),*args],check=True,capture_output=True)
            git('init','-q');git('config','user.name','Fixture');git('config','user.email','fixture@example.invalid')
            for name in builder.PRODUCT_FILES|builder.ENCYCLOPEDIA_REQUIRED|{'catalog/tools.json','STATE.md','CHANGELOG.txt','tests/private.py','docs/CHANGELOG.md'}:
                p=root/name;p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);p.write_text('1.0.0' if name=='VERSION' else 'original')
            git('add','.');git('commit','-qm','fixture')
            (root/'docs/private.txt').write_text('untracked private');(root/'sandbox').mkdir();(root/'sandbox/secret.txt').write_text('private')
            (root/'README.md').write_text('uncommitted change')
            a=root/'a.zip';b=root/'b.zip'
            receipt=builder.build(root,a);builder.build(root,b)
            self.assertEqual(a.read_bytes(),b.read_bytes())
            with zipfile.ZipFile(a) as archive:
                prefix='Reverse-This-1.0.0/'
                names={n.removeprefix(prefix) for n in archive.namelist()}
                self.assertEqual(names,builder.PRODUCT_FILES|builder.ENCYCLOPEDIA_REQUIRED|{'catalog/tools.json','RELEASE.json'})
                self.assertEqual(archive.read(prefix+'README.md'),b'original')
                manifest=json.loads(archive.read(prefix+'RELEASE.json'))
                self.assertEqual(manifest['commit'],receipt['commit'])
                for row in manifest['files']:
                    self.assertEqual(row['sha256'],hashlib.sha256(archive.read(prefix+row['path'])).hexdigest())
            before=a.read_bytes()
            with self.assertRaises(FileExistsError):builder.build(root,a)
            self.assertEqual(a.read_bytes(),before)

class ReleaseWorkflowTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_only_a_green_untagged_main_sha_publishes_and_the_job_never_skips(self):
        wf=(ROOT/'.github/workflows/release.yml').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        for gate in ("workflows: [ci]","conclusion == 'success'","event == 'push'",'if [ "$main" != "$SHA" ]',
                     'git ls-remote --tags origin','r["name"] != "Publish release"','sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS.txt','actions/attest-build-provenance@'):
            self.assertIn(gate,wf)
        # A skipped run is filed under main's newest commit and fails its exact-SHA verdict.
        self.assertNotIn('\n    if: github.event.workflow_run',wf)
        self.assertIn('if [ "$CI_GREEN" != "true" ]',wf)
        for forbidden in ('gh release delete','--clobber','git push --force','git tag -f'):self.assertNotIn(forbidden,wf)

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

#Requires -Version 5.1
# Offline .NET prerequisite probes; fake winget never installs machine software.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$root = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
$tokens = $null
$parseErrors = $null
$ast = [System.Management.Automation.Language.Parser]::ParseFile(
    (Join-Path $root 'Install-Modkit.ps1'), [ref]$tokens, [ref]$parseErrors)
if ($parseErrors.Count) { throw ($parseErrors | Out-String) }
foreach ($definition in $ast.FindAll({ param($node)
    $node -is [System.Management.Automation.Language.FunctionDefinitionAst]
}, $false)) { Invoke-Expression $definition.Extent.Text }
$productionProbe = (Get-Item Function:\Test-DotNet10).ScriptBlock
$productionFind = (Get-Item Function:\Find-DotNet10).ScriptBlock

function Assert-True {
    param([bool]$Condition, [string]$Message)
    if (-not $Condition) { throw $Message }
}
function Assert-Throws {
    param([scriptblock]$Action, [string]$Pattern)
    $failure = $null
    try { & $Action | Out-Null } catch { $failure = $_.Exception.Message }
    Assert-True ([bool]$failure -and $failure -match $Pattern) "Expected failure matching '$Pattern'; got '$failure'."
}
function Write-TestFile {
    param([string]$Path, [string]$Text)
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($Path, $Text, (New-Object Text.UTF8Encoding $false))
}
function Write-Log { param([string]$Message, [switch]$Detail) }
# Resolve only explicit mock commands. No test can reach the real winget.
function Get-Command {
    [CmdletBinding()]
    param([string]$Name, $CommandType)
    if ($Name -eq 'dotnet' -and $script:MockDotNet) { return [pscustomobject]@{ Source = $script:MockDotNet } }
    if ($Name -eq 'winget' -and $script:MockWinget) { return [pscustomobject]@{ Source = $script:MockWinget } }
}

$testBase = Join-Path $root 'sandbox\tmp'

$savedEnvironment = @{}

try {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $testRoot | Out-Null
    $fake = Join-Path $testRoot 'selected dotnet.cmd'
    $fakeWinget = Join-Path $testRoot 'fake winget.cmd'

@echo off
setlocal DisableDelayedExpansion
if "%~1"=="--list-sdks" (type "%~dp0sdks.txt" & exit /b 0)
if "%~1"=="--list-runtimes" (type "%~dp0runtimes.txt" & exit /b 0)
>> "%~dp0dotnet-calls.txt" echo %*
if "%~2"=="install" exit /b %RT_DOTNET_INSTALL_EXIT%
if "%~2"=="update" exit /b %RT_DOTNET_UPDATE_EXIT%
if "%~2"=="list" (
  echo ilspycmd 11.1.0.9782 ilspycmd
  exit /b %RT_DOTNET_LIST_EXIT%
)
exit /b 99
'@
    Write-TestFile $fakeWinget @'
@echo off
setlocal DisableDelayedExpansion
> "%~dp0winget-calls.txt" echo %*
> "%~dp0winget-ran.txt" echo present
exit /b %RT_WINGET_EXIT%
'@
    $sdkFile = Join-Path $testRoot 'sdks.txt'
    $runtimeFile = Join-Path $testRoot 'runtimes.txt'
    Write-TestFile $sdkFile "8.0.418 [owned sdk]`r`n"
    Write-TestFile $runtimeFile "Microsoft.NETCore.App 9.0.20 [owned runtime]`r`n"
    Assert-True (-not (Test-DotNet10 $fake)) 'SDK8/runtime9 satisfied a net10 prerequisite.'
    Write-TestFile $sdkFile "10.0.301 [owned sdk]`r`n"
    Assert-True (-not (Test-DotNet10 $fake)) 'SDK10 with a missing runtime10 was accepted.'
    Write-TestFile $sdkFile "10.0.100-preview.7 [owned sdk]`r`n"
    Write-TestFile $runtimeFile "Microsoft.NETCore.App 10.0.9 [owned runtime]`r`n"
    Assert-True (-not (Test-DotNet10 $fake)) 'A preview-only SDK was accepted.'
    Write-TestFile $sdkFile "10.0.301 [owned sdk]`r`n"
    Assert-True (Test-DotNet10 $fake) 'Stable SDK10 plus runtime10 was rejected.'
    Assert-True (-not (Test-DotNet10 (Join-Path $testRoot 'missing.exe'))) 'A missing executable passed the probe.'

    $env:DOTNET_ROOT_X64 = Join-Path $testRoot 'old x64'
    $env:DOTNET_ROOT = Join-Path $testRoot 'portable ten'
    $script:MockDotNet = Join-Path $testRoot 'stale PATH\dotnet.exe'
    $script:AcceptHost = Join-Path $env:DOTNET_ROOT 'dotnet.exe'
    $script:ProbedHosts = @()
    function Test-DotNet10 { param([string]$Executable)
        $script:ProbedHosts += $Executable
        return $Executable -eq $script:AcceptHost
    }
    Assert-True ((Find-DotNet10) -eq $script:AcceptHost) 'Portable DOTNET_ROOT was ignored after an unsuitable X64 root.'
    $script:AcceptHost = Join-Path $env:ProgramFiles 'dotnet\dotnet.exe'
    Assert-True ((Find-DotNet10) -eq $script:AcceptHost) 'A stale PATH blocked the Program Files host.'
    Assert-True ($script:ProbedHosts -contains $script:MockDotNet) 'The PATH host was not considered.'
    $script:AcceptHost = $script:MockDotNet
    Assert-True ((Find-DotNet10) -eq $script:MockDotNet) 'A suitable PATH host was ignored.'

    $script:PrerequisitePresent = $false
    function Find-DotNet10 {
        if ($script:PrerequisitePresent -or (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $testRoot 'winget-ran.txt'))) { return $fake }
        return $null

    $script:MockWinget = $fakeWinget

    Assert-Throws { Initialize-DotNet10 } 'Microsoft.DotNet.SDK.10 failed \(23\)'
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $testRoot 'winget-ran.txt')
    $env:RT_WINGET_EXIT = '0'
    Assert-True ((Initialize-DotNet10) -eq $fake) 'The newly installed host was not selected.'
    Assert-True ($script:DotNetExe -eq $fake -and $script:DotNetHome -eq $testRoot) 'Runtime selection was not recorded for shims.'
    $wingetCall = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $testRoot 'winget-calls.txt') -Raw
    Assert-True ($wingetCall -match '--id Microsoft.DotNet.SDK.10 -e' -and $wingetCall -match '--disable-interactivity') 'The official exact SDK package was not requested.'
    function Find-DotNet10 { return $null }
    Assert-Throws { Initialize-DotNet10 } 'SDK and runtime were not found'
    function Find-DotNet10 { return $fake }
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $testRoot 'winget-calls.txt')
    [void](Initialize-DotNet10)
    Assert-True (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $testRoot 'winget-calls.txt'))) 'An existing suitable host invoked winget.'

    $ToolsDir = Join-Path $testRoot 'tools'
    $BinDir = Join-Path $testRoot 'bin'
    $KitRoot = $testRoot
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ToolsDir, $BinDir | Out-Null
    $tool = [pscustomobject]@{ id = 'ilspycmd'; method = 'dotnet-tool'; package = 'ilspycmd'; shims = @([pscustomobject]@{ name = 'ilspycmd'; exe_glob = 'ilspycmd.exe' }) }
    function Test-AlreadyInstalled { param([string]$Dest, [string]$Marker) return $false }
    function Install-Shims { param($Tool, [string]$Dest) return @() }
    function Write-Marker { param([string]$Dest, [string]$Marker) }
    $env:RT_DOTNET_INSTALL_EXIT = '1'
    $env:RT_DOTNET_UPDATE_EXIT = '0'
    $env:RT_DOTNET_LIST_EXIT = '0'
    $row = Install-DotNetTool $tool
    Assert-True ($row -isnot [array] -and $row.version -eq '11.1.0.9782') 'Selected-host stdout polluted the installed record.'
    $calls = @(Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $testRoot 'dotnet-calls.txt'))
    Assert-True ($calls.Count -eq 3 -and $calls[0] -match '^tool install ' -and $calls[1] -match '^tool update ' -and $calls[2] -match '^tool list ') 'Install/update/list did not all use the selected host.'
    Assert-True ($calls[0] -match '--framework net10\.0(?:\s|$)' -and $calls[1] -match '--framework net10\.0(?:\s|$)') 'Install/update must explicitly target net10.0 despite a caller SDK pin.'
    $env:RT_DOTNET_LIST_EXIT = '17'
    Assert-Throws { Install-DotNetTool $tool } 'dotnet tool list failed \(17\)'
    $env:RT_DOTNET_UPDATE_EXIT = '19'
    Assert-Throws { Install-DotNetTool $tool } 'dotnet tool ilspycmd failed \(19\)'
    $target = Join-Path $ToolsDir 'runtime-probe.cmd'
    Write-TestFile $target @'
@echo off
> "%~dp0runtime-root.txt" echo %DOTNET_ROOT%
>> "%~dp0runtime-root.txt" echo %DOTNET_ROOT_X64%
exit /b 0
'@
    $shim = Write-Shim -Name 'runtime-probe' -Target $target -DotNetHome $testRoot
    $beforeRoot = $env:DOTNET_ROOT
    $beforeRootX64 = $env:DOTNET_ROOT_X64
    & $shim
    Assert-True ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) 'A shim with the selected runtime failed.'
    $runtimeRoots = @(Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $ToolsDir 'runtime-root.txt'))
    Assert-True ($runtimeRoots.Count -eq 2 -and $runtimeRoots[0] -eq $testRoot -and $runtimeRoots[1] -eq $testRoot) 'The apphost shim did not bind the selected runtime.'
    Assert-True ($env:DOTNET_ROOT -ceq $beforeRoot -and $env:DOTNET_ROOT_X64 -ceq $beforeRootX64) 'A runtime shim changed its caller environment.'
    Assert-True ($env:PATH -ceq $savedPath -and $ErrorActionPreference -eq $savedPreference) 'Prerequisite handling changed PATH or error policy.'
    Write-Host 'dotnet prerequisite ok: stable SDK/runtime10, portable discovery, winget failures and rediscovery, selected-host tool commands'
} finally {
    foreach ($name in $environmentNames) {
        if ($null -eq $savedEnvironment[$name]) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath ('Env:\' + $name) -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
        else { [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable($name, $savedEnvironment[$name], 'Process') }
    }
    Set-Item Function:\Test-DotNet10 $productionProbe
    Set-Item Function:\Find-DotNet10 $productionFind
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $testRoot) {
        $resolved = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $testRoot).Path
        $prefix = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($testBase).TrimEnd('\') + '\'
        if (-not $resolved.StartsWith($prefix, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'Refusing cleanup outside test root.' }
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $testRoot -Recurse -Force
    }
}

`tests/test_encyclopedia.py`:

```py
"""Encyclopedia contract. Stdlib only. Run: python tests/test_encyclopedia.py"""

import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
REQUIRED = [
    "context7.json",
    "encyclopedia/README.md",
    "encyclopedia/ROUTING.md",
    "encyclopedia/CLAIMS.md",
    "encyclopedia/anticheat-risk.md",
    "encyclopedia/VALIDATION.md",
    "encyclopedia/engines/creation-engine.md",
    "encyclopedia/engines/unreal-3-4-5.md",
    "encyclopedia/engines/unity-mono-il2cpp.md",
    "encyclopedia/engines/rage-gta5.md",
    "encyclopedia/engines/pokemon-switch.md",
    "encyclopedia/blueprints/ipc_seqlock.h",
    "encyclopedia/blueprints/ipc_seqlock.py",
    "encyclopedia/blueprints/passthrough_reproject.fxh",
    "encyclopedia/blueprints/fake_host_d3d11.cpp",
    "encyclopedia/blueprints/ipc_link_test.cpp",
    "encyclopedia/blueprints/proxy_entity_bridge.md",
    "encyclopedia/re-recipes/ghidra-mcp-streamable-http.md",
    "encyclopedia/re-recipes/frida-downstream-sink.md",
    "encyclopedia/re-recipes/aob-wildcard-signatures.md",
    "encyclopedia/games/TEMPLATE.md",
    "encyclopedia/games/arkham-knight-x-spiderman.md",
    "encyclopedia/games/gtav-x-minecraft-passthrough.md",
    "encyclopedia/ERROR-REGISTRY.json",
    "kit/README.md",
    "kit/pe_recon.py",
    "kit/aob_gen.py",
    "kit/ghidra_mcp_up.py",
]


def main():
    errors = []
    for rel in REQUIRED:
        path = ROOT / rel
        if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size == 0:
            errors.append("missing or empty: " + rel)

    try:
        manifest = json.loads((ROOT / "context7.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - report as a contract error
        errors.append("context7.json does not parse: " + str(exc))
        manifest = {}
    if manifest.get("$schema") != "https://context7.com/schema/context7.json":
        errors.append("context7.json has no Context7 $schema")
    if manifest.get("folders") != ["encyclopedia"]:
        errors.append("context7.json folders must be ['encyclopedia']")
    if not manifest.get("projectTitle") or not manifest.get("description"):
        errors.append("context7.json is missing projectTitle or description")
    if not isinstance(manifest.get("rules"), list) or not manifest["rules"]:
        errors.append("context7.json has no rules")
    if not any("scoped" in rule and "version" in rule for rule in manifest.get("rules", [])):
        errors.append("context7.json needs a rule that scopes limits to a tool and version")

    claims = (ROOT / "encyclopedia/CLAIMS.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower()
    for needle in ("escape hatch", "re-test", "version"):
        if needle not in claims:
            errors.append("CLAIMS.md is missing " + needle)
    anti = (ROOT / "encyclopedia/anticheat-risk.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower()
    for needle in ("risk tier", "policy", "cosmetic", "marvelrivals.com", "monsterhunter.com"):
        if needle not in anti:
            errors.append("anticheat-risk.md is missing " + needle)
    banned = ("permanently impossible", "never be possible", "no way to", "impossible to", "can never")
    for path in [ROOT / "README.md", ROOT / "AGENTS.md"] + list((ROOT / "encyclopedia").rglob("*.md")):
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower()
        for phrase in banned:
            if phrase in text:
                errors.append("{0} uses a permanent capability block: {1}".format(path.relative_to(ROOT), phrase))
    for folder in manifest.get("folders", []):
        if not (ROOT / folder).is_dir():
            errors.append("context7 folder does not exist: " + folder)

    try:
        registry = json.loads((ROOT / "encyclopedia/ERROR-REGISTRY.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        errors.append("ERROR-REGISTRY.json does not parse: " + str(exc))
        registry = {}
    for row in registry.get("errors", []):
        for field in ("id", "signature", "cause", "fix"):
            if not row.get(field):
                errors.append("ERROR-REGISTRY row {0} is missing {1}".format(row.get("id", "?"), field))

    for card in ("creation-engine.md", "unreal-3-4-5.md", "unity-mono-il2cpp.md", "rage-gta5.md"):
        text = (ROOT / "encyclopedia/engines" / card).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for heading in ("## Coordinates", "## Depth", "## Verification"):
            if heading not in text:
                errors.append("{0} is missing {1}".format(card, heading))

    template = (ROOT / "encyclopedia/games/TEMPLATE.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower()
    for needle in ("build id", "exe sha256", "loader version", "coordinate conversion", "known engine traps"):
        if needle not in template:
            errors.append("games/TEMPLATE.md is missing " + needle)

    link = re.compile(r"\[[^\]]+\]\(([^)]+)\)")
    for path in [ROOT / rel for rel in REQUIRED if rel.endswith(".md")]:
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for target in link.findall(text):
            target = target.split("#", 1)[0].strip()
            if not target or target.startswith(("http://", "https://", "mailto:", "MEDIA:")):
                continue
            resolved = (path.parent / target).resolve()
            if not resolved.exists():
                errors.append("{0} has a broken link: {1}".format(path.relative_to(ROOT), target))

    if errors:
        print("\n".join(errors))
        return 1
    print("encyclopedia ok: {0} files, {1} registry rows".format(len(REQUIRED), len(registry.get("errors", []))))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

`tests/test_entrypoint.ps1`:

```ps1
#Requires -Version 5.1
# Exercise the real batch entrypoint; all failure fixtures stay below sandbox/tmp.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$root = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
$testBase = Join-Path $root 'sandbox\tmp'
$testRoot = Join-Path $testBase ('entrypoint-tests-' + [Guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))

function Assert-True {
    param([bool]$Condition, [string]$Message)
    if (-not $Condition) { throw $Message }
}

function Invoke-Entrypoint {
    param([string]$Directory, [string]$Arguments, [string]$DelayedExpansion = 'off')
    $info = New-Object System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
    $info.FileName = $env:ComSpec
    $info.Arguments = '/d /v:' + $DelayedExpansion + ' /c .\install.cmd ' + $Arguments
    $info.WorkingDirectory = $Directory
    $info.UseShellExecute = $false
    $info.CreateNoWindow = $true
    $info.RedirectStandardInput = $true
    $info.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
    $info.RedirectStandardError = $true
    $process = New-Object System.Diagnostics.Process
    $process.StartInfo = $info
    try {
        [void]$process.Start()
        $process.StandardInput.Close()
        $stdout = $process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync()
        $stderr = $process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync()
        if (-not $process.WaitForExit(15000)) {
            $process.Kill()
            throw 'Batch entrypoint did not finish with stdin closed.'
        }
        return [pscustomobject]@{
            ExitCode = $process.ExitCode
            Output = $stdout.GetAwaiter().GetResult()
            Error = $stderr.GetAwaiter().GetResult()
        }
    } finally {
        $process.Dispose()
    }
}

try {
    foreach ($mode in @('-Plan', '-SelfTest')) {
        $result = Invoke-Entrypoint -Directory $root -Arguments $mode
        Assert-True ($result.ExitCode -eq 0) "Real batch entrypoint failed $mode`: $($result.Error)"
        Assert-True ($result.Output -notmatch 'Modkit ready') 'Read-only checks must not claim tools are ready.'
        Assert-True ($result.Output -notmatch 'Press any key') 'Argument-driven runs must not pause.'
    }

    $fixture = Join-Path $testRoot ('kit & ' + [char]0x4e2d + ' ! path')
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $fixture | Out-Null
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $root 'install.cmd') -Destination (Join-Path $fixture 'install.cmd')
    $probe = @'
param([int]$Result = 0, [string]$Value = '')
Write-Host "PROBE_RESULT=$Result"
Write-Host "PROBE_VALUE=$Value"
exit $Result
'@
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $fixture 'Install-Modkit.ps1'), $probe)
    foreach ($expected in @(0, 1, 27, -1)) {
        $result = Invoke-Entrypoint -Directory $fixture -Arguments ('-Result ' + $expected)
        Assert-True ($result.ExitCode -eq $expected) "Batch lost child exit code $expected (got $($result.ExitCode))."
        Assert-True ($result.Output -match ('PROBE_RESULT=' + $expected)) 'The fixture installer did not run.'
        if ($expected -ne 0) {
            Assert-True ($result.Output -notmatch 'Modkit command completed|Modkit ready') 'A failed child was reported as success.'
        }
    }
    $result = Invoke-Entrypoint -Directory $fixture -Arguments '-Result 0' -DelayedExpansion 'on'
    Assert-True ($result.ExitCode -eq 0 -and $result.Output -match 'PROBE_RESULT=0') 'Inherited delayed expansion broke the path containing !.'
    $result = Invoke-Entrypoint -Directory $fixture -Arguments '-Value "spaces & punctuation ! intact"'
    Assert-True ($result.ExitCode -eq 0 -and $result.Output -match 'PROBE_VALUE=spaces & punctuation ! intact') 'Quoted arguments were not preserved.'
    $result = Invoke-Entrypoint -Directory $fixture -Arguments ''
    Assert-True ($result.ExitCode -eq 0) 'Interactive-mode PAUSE changed successful child status with stdin closed.'

    Remove-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $fixture 'Install-Modkit.ps1')
    $result = Invoke-Entrypoint -Directory $fixture -Arguments '-Plan'
    Assert-True ($result.ExitCode -ne 0) 'A missing installer script was reported as success.'
    Assert-True ($result.Output -notmatch 'Modkit command completed|Modkit ready') 'PowerShell startup failure printed success.'
    Write-Host 'entrypoint ok: real Plan/SelfTest, exact exit status, closed stdin, punctuation paths and quoted arguments'
} finally {
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $testRoot) {
        $resolved = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $testRoot).Path
        $prefix = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($testBase).TrimEnd('\') + '\'
        if (-not $resolved.StartsWith($prefix, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {
            throw "Refusing cleanup outside test root: $resolved"
        }
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $testRoot -Recurse -Force
    }
}

```

`tests/test_installer.ps1`:

```ps1
#Requires -Version 5.1
<## Offline installer regression checks. No downloads or machine configuration writes. ##>
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$sourceRoot = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
$tokens = $null
$parseErrors = $null
$ast = [System.Management.Automation.Language.Parser]::ParseFile(
    (Join-Path $sourceRoot 'Install-Modkit.ps1'), [ref]$tokens, [ref]$parseErrors)
if ($parseErrors.Count) { throw ($parseErrors | Out-String) }
foreach ($definition in $ast.FindAll({
    param($node)
    $node -is [System.Management.Automation.Language.FunctionDefinitionAst]
}, $false)) {
    Invoke-Expression $definition.Extent.Text
}

function Assert-True {
    param([bool]$Condition, [string]$Message)
    if (-not $Condition) { throw $Message }
}

$testBase = Join-Path $sourceRoot 'sandbox\tmp'
$testRoot = Join-Path $testBase ('tests-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
$KitRoot = $testRoot
$Sandbox = Join-Path $testRoot 'sandbox'
$BinDir = Join-Path $Sandbox 'bin'
$ToolsDir = Join-Path $Sandbox 'tools'
$TmpDir = Join-Path $Sandbox 'tmp'
$LogDir = Join-Path $Sandbox 'log'
$LogPath = Join-Path $LogDir 'modkit.log'
$StatePath = Join-Path $Sandbox 'installed.json'
$PointerDir = Join-Path $testRoot 'profile-pointer'
$PointerPath = Join-Path $PointerDir 'kit.json'
$kitVersion = '0.0.0'
$Force = $false
$script:JavaHome = $null
$script:ExtraInstalled = @()
$tool = [pscustomobject]@{
    id = 'probe'
    method = 'github-release'
    needs_jdk = $null
    shims = @([pscustomobject]@{ name = 'probe'; exe_glob = 'probe.cmd'; prefer_path = $null; require_path = $null })
}
$script:CurrentTool = $tool
$script:InstallState = [pscustomobject]@{
    schema = 1; kit_version = $kitVersion; kit_root = $KitRoot; java_home = $null
    tools = [pscustomobject]@{}
}

try {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BinDir, $ToolsDir, $TmpDir, $LogDir, $PointerDir -Force | Out-Null
    $dest = Join-Path $ToolsDir 'probe'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dest | Out-Null
    Write-Utf8 -Path (Join-Path $dest 'old.txt') -Text 'known-good'
    Write-Utf8 -Path (Join-Path $dest 'probe.cmd') -Text "@echo off`r`nexit /b 0`r`n"
    $stage = Join-Path $TmpDir 'bad-stage'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $stage | Out-Null
    Write-Utf8 -Path (Join-Path $stage 'unexpected.txt') -Text 'not the required executable'
    $threw = $false
    try { Move-FlattenedStage -Stage $stage -Dest $dest | Out-Null } catch { $threw = $true }
    Assert-True $threw 'A payload missing its required shim was accepted'
    Assert-True ((Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dest 'old.txt') -Raw) -eq 'known-good') 'An invalid update destroyed the previous payload'

    $stage = Join-Path $TmpDir 'good-stage'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $stage | Out-Null
    Write-Utf8 -Path (Join-Path $stage 'probe.cmd') -Text "@echo off`r`nexit /b 0`r`n"
    $helperDir = Join-Path $stage 'x86'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $helperDir | Out-Null
    Write-Utf8 -Path (Join-Path $helperDir 'probe.cmd') -Text 'other architecture helper'
    Assert-True ((Find-ToolFile -Root $stage -Glob 'probe.cmd').FullName -eq (Join-Path $stage 'probe.cmd')) 'A nested architecture helper displaced the main executable'
    Move-FlattenedStage -Stage $stage -Dest $dest | Out-Null
    Assert-True (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dest 'probe.cmd')) 'A valid payload was not installed'
    Assert-True (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dest 'old.txt'))) 'An update merged stale files into the new payload'

    $seven = Join-Path $TmpDir 'fake-7z.cmd'
    Write-Utf8 -Path $seven -Text "@echo off`r`necho archive progress`r`nexit /b 0`r`n"
    $output = @(Invoke-SevenZip -SevenZip $seven -ArgumentList @('x', 'dummy.zip'))
    Assert-True ($output.Count -eq 0) 'Archive progress polluted the installer return value'
    Write-Utf8 -Path $seven -Text "@echo off`r`necho failed archive`r`nexit /b 3`r`n"
    $threw = $false
    try { Invoke-SevenZip -SevenZip $seven -ArgumentList @('x') | Out-Null } catch { $threw = $true }
    Assert-True $threw 'A failing archive process was accepted'

    $caller = Join-Path $testRoot 'caller directory'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $caller | Out-Null
    Write-Utf8 -Path (Join-Path $caller 'relative-input.txt') -Text 'caller input'
    $result = Join-Path $TmpDir 'shim-result.txt'
    $target = Join-Path $dest 'probe.cmd'
    Write-Utf8 -Path $target -Text (@(
        '@echo off'
        '> "%RT_TEST_RESULT%" echo argument=%~1'
        '>> "%RT_TEST_RESULT%" echo duringjava=%JAVA_HOME%'
        '>> "%RT_TEST_RESULT%" echo duringdotnet=%DOTNET_ROOT%'
        '>> "%RT_TEST_RESULT%" echo duringdotnetx64=%DOTNET_ROOT_X64%'
        '>> "%RT_TEST_RESULT%" echo duringcwd=%CD%'
        'if exist relative-input.txt >> "%RT_TEST_RESULT%" echo relative=present'
        'exit /b 7'
    ) -join "`r`n")
    $java = Join-Path $testRoot 'fake jdk!%RT_KIT_MARKER%'
    $dotnetHome = Join-Path $testRoot 'fake dotnet!%RT_KIT_MARKER%'
    $shim = Write-Shim -Name 'probe' -Target $target -JavaHome $java -DotNetHome $dotnetHome
    $parent = Join-Path $TmpDir 'shim-caller.cmd'
    Write-Utf8 -Path $parent -Text (@(
        '@echo off'
        'set "JAVA_HOME=caller-java"'
        'set "DOTNET_ROOT=caller-dotnet"'
        'set "DOTNET_ROOT_X64=caller-dotnet-x64"'
        ('set "RT_TEST_RESULT=' + $result + '"')
        ('cd /d "' + $caller + '"')
        ('call "' + $shim + '" "argument with spaces"')
        'set "RT_TEST_EXIT=%ERRORLEVEL%"'
        '>> "%RT_TEST_RESULT%" echo aftercwd=%CD%'
        '>> "%RT_TEST_RESULT%" echo afterjava=%JAVA_HOME%'
        '>> "%RT_TEST_RESULT%" echo afterdotnet=%DOTNET_ROOT%'
        '>> "%RT_TEST_RESULT%" echo afterdotnetx64=%DOTNET_ROOT_X64%'
        '>> "%RT_TEST_RESULT%" echo exit=%RT_TEST_EXIT%'
        'exit /b 0'
    ) -join "`r`n")
    & $parent
    Assert-True ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) 'The shim caller did not finish'
    $lines = @(Get-Content -LiteralPath $result)
    Assert-True ($lines -contains 'argument=argument with spaces') 'A shim damaged quoted arguments'
    Assert-True ($lines -contains ('duringjava=' + $java)) 'A shim did not set its private JDK'
    Assert-True ($lines -contains ('duringdotnet=' + $dotnetHome) -and $lines -contains ('duringdotnetx64=' + $dotnetHome)) 'A shim did not set its private .NET runtime'
    Assert-True ($lines -contains ('duringcwd=' + $caller)) 'A CLI shim changed the working directory used for relative input paths'
    Assert-True ($lines -contains 'relative=present') 'A CLI shim could not see an input file relative to its caller'
    Assert-True ($lines -contains ('aftercwd=' + $caller)) 'A shim changed its caller working directory'
    Assert-True ($lines -contains 'afterjava=caller-java') 'A shim leaked environment changes into its caller'
    Assert-True ($lines -contains 'afterdotnet=caller-dotnet' -and $lines -contains 'afterdotnetx64=caller-dotnet-x64') 'A shim leaked .NET runtime settings into its caller'
    Assert-True ($lines -contains 'exit=7') 'A shim lost the tool exit code'

    $record = [pscustomobject]@{
        id = 'probe'; version = 'v1'; asset = 'probe.zip'; sha256 = ('a' * 64)
        hash_source = 'computed'; dir = 'sandbox/tools/probe'; shims = [pscustomobject]@{ probe = 'sandbox/bin/probe.cmd' }
    }
    $script:InstallState.tools | Add-Member -NotePropertyName probe -NotePropertyValue $record -Force
    Write-Marker -Dest $dest -Marker 'v1|probe.zip'
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $shim -Force
    $cached = Test-AlreadyInstalled -Dest $dest -Marker 'v1|probe.zip'
    Assert-True $cached 'A complete cached payload was not reused'
    Assert-True (Test-Path -LiteralPath $shim) 'A cached install did not recreate its missing shim'

    $legacy = [pscustomobject]@{
        schema = 1; kit_version = $kitVersion; kit_root = $KitRoot; java_home = $null
        tools = [pscustomobject]@{ probe = @('archive progress', $record) }
    }
    Write-Utf8 -Path $StatePath -Text ($legacy | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8)
    $loaded = New-State
    Assert-True ($loaded.tools.probe -isnot [array]) 'Legacy progress strings were not removed from installed state'
    Assert-True ($loaded.tools.probe.id -eq 'probe') 'State repair lost the valid tool record'

    $unrelated = 'a' * 64
    $release = [pscustomobject]@{ assets = @(); body = "Linux archive SHA256: $unrelated" }
    Assert-True ($null -eq (Get-PublishedHash -Release $release -AssetName 'probe.zip')) 'A release body hash was incorrectly applied to another asset'
    $expected = 'b' * 64
    $release.assets = @([pscustomobject]@{ name = 'probe.zip'; digest = ('sha256:' + $expected) })
    $published = Get-PublishedHash -Release $release -AssetName 'probe.zip'
    Assert-True ($published.hash -eq $expected -and $published.source -eq 'github-digest') 'The exact asset digest was not selected'
    Assert-True ($null -eq (Get-PublishedHash -Release $release -AssetName 'other.zip')) 'An asset digest was incorrectly applied to another filename'
    $commit = 'c' * 40
    $url = Get-GitHubArchiveUrl -Repo 'owner/repo' -Kind 'commit' -Ref $commit
    Assert-True ($url -eq "https://github.com/owner/repo/archive/$commit.zip") 'Source archive URL did not pin the recorded commit'
    $threw = $false
    try { Get-GitHubArchiveUrl -Repo 'owner/repo' -Kind 'commit' -Ref 'main' | Out-Null } catch { $threw = $true }
    Assert-True $threw 'A mutable branch was accepted as a commit archive'

    $atomic = Join-Path $TmpDir 'atomic.json'
    Write-AtomicJson -Path $atomic -Text '{"phase":"old"}'
    $threw = $false
    try { Write-AtomicJson -Path $atomic -Text '{broken' } catch { $threw = $true }
    Assert-True $threw 'An invalid state document was accepted'
    Assert-True ((Get-Content -LiteralPath $atomic -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).phase -eq 'old') 'A failed state update damaged the last valid document'
    Write-AtomicJson -Path $atomic -Text '{"phase":"new"}'
    Assert-True ((Get-Content -LiteralPath $atomic -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).phase -eq 'new') 'Atomic state replacement did not write the new document'
    Assert-True (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath ($atomic + '.new'))) 'Atomic state replacement left a temporary document behind'

    # Use a real Windows handle denying delete sharing, then release it independently.
    Add-Type -TypeDefinition @'
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
public static class AtomicJsonLockFixture {
    public static Task Hold(string path, int milliseconds) {
        var handle = File.Open(path, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.Read);
        return Task.Run(() => { try { Thread.Sleep(milliseconds); } finally { handle.Dispose(); } });
    }
    public static IOException Error(int code) {
        return new IOException("Injected Win32 " + code, unchecked((int)0x80070000) | code);
    }
}
'@
    $releaseLock = [AtomicJsonLockFixture]::Hold($atomic, 400)
    try {
        Write-AtomicJson -Path $atomic -Text '{"phase":"lock-released"}'
        Assert-True ((Get-Content -LiteralPath $atomic -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).phase -eq 'lock-released') 'Atomic replacement did not recover after a real sharing lock released'
        Assert-True (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath ($atomic + '.new'))) 'A recovered sharing lock left the new document uncommitted'
    } finally { $releaseLock.Wait() }
    $heldLock = [IO.File]::Open($atomic, [IO.FileMode]::Open, [IO.FileAccess]::Read, [IO.FileShare]::Read)
    try {
        $failure = $null
        try { Write-AtomicJson -Path $atomic -Text '{"phase":"pending"}' } catch { $failure = $_.Exception }
        while ($failure -and $failure -isnot [IO.IOException]) { $failure = $failure.InnerException }
        Assert-True ($null -ne $failure -and ($failure.HResult -band 0xFFFF) -eq 32) 'An exhausted real sharing lock did not surface its IOException'
        Assert-True ((Get-Content -LiteralPath $atomic -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).phase -eq 'lock-released') 'An exhausted sharing lock changed the good target'
        Assert-True ((Get-Content -LiteralPath ($atomic + '.new') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).phase -eq 'pending') 'An exhausted sharing lock lost the pending replacement'
    } finally { $heldLock.Dispose() }

    # Inject only the native replace boundary to deterministically cover Win32 1175 and bounds.
    $savedAtomicWriter = ${function:Write-AtomicJson}
    try {
        $probeBody = $savedAtomicWriter.ToString().Replace(
            '[IO.File]::Replace($temp, $Path, [NullString]::Value)',
            'Invoke-AtomicReplaceFixture -Source $temp -Target $Path')
        Assert-True ($probeBody -ne $savedAtomicWriter.ToString()) 'Atomic replace fixture did not intercept the native boundary'
        Set-Item Function:\Write-AtomicJson -Value ([scriptblock]::Create($probeBody))
        function Invoke-AtomicReplaceFixture {
            param([string]$Source, [string]$Target)
            $script:AtomicAttempts++
            if ($script:AtomicAttempts -le $script:AtomicFailures) { throw [AtomicJsonLockFixture]::Error($script:AtomicErrorCode) }
            [IO.File]::Replace($Source, $Target, [NullString]::Value)
        }
        $script:AtomicAttempts = 0
        $script:AtomicFailures = 2
        $script:AtomicErrorCode = 1175
        Write-AtomicJson -Path $atomic -Text '{"phase":"retry-success"}'
        Assert-True ($script:AtomicAttempts -eq 3 -and (Get-Content -LiteralPath $atomic -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).phase -eq 'retry-success') 'An unchanged-files Win32 1175 failure was not retried successfully'
        foreach ($code in @(1175, 5, 1176, 1177)) {
            $script:AtomicAttempts = 0
            $script:AtomicFailures = 99
            $script:AtomicErrorCode = $code
            $failure = $null
            try { Write-AtomicJson -Path $atomic -Text '{"phase":"pending-failure"}' } catch { $failure = $_.Exception }
            while ($failure -and $failure -isnot [IO.IOException]) { $failure = $failure.InnerException }
            $expectedAttempts = if ($code -eq 1175) { 6 } else { 1 }
            Assert-True ($script:AtomicAttempts -eq $expectedAttempts -and $failure -and ($failure.HResult -band 0xFFFF) -eq $code) ('Atomic replacement did not bound or surface Win32 ' + $code)
            Assert-True ((Get-Content -LiteralPath $atomic -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).phase -eq 'retry-success') ('A failed Win32 ' + $code + ' update damaged the good target')
            Assert-True ((Get-Content -LiteralPath ($atomic + '.new') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).phase -eq 'pending-failure') ('A failed Win32 ' + $code + ' update lost its new document')
        }
    } finally {
        Set-Item Function:\Write-AtomicJson -Value $savedAtomicWriter
        Remove-Item Function:\Invoke-AtomicReplaceFixture
    }
    Write-AtomicJson -Path $atomic -Text '{"phase":"final-recovery"}'
    Assert-True ((Get-Content -LiteralPath $atomic -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).phase -eq 'final-recovery') 'A later atomic write did not recover after lock exhaustion'

    $savedRelease = ${function:Get-GitHubRelease}
    $savedJdk = ${function:Install-Jdk}
    $savedDownload = ${function:Save-Url}
    try {
        function Get-GitHubRelease {
            return [pscustomobject]@{ tag_name = 'v1'; assets = @([pscustomobject]@{ name = 'probe.zip' }) }
        }
        function Install-Jdk { return $script:FakeJavaHome }
        function Save-Url { throw 'A cached install unexpectedly attempted a download' }
        $tool | Add-Member -NotePropertyName repo -NotePropertyValue 'fixture/repo' -Force
        $tool | Add-Member -NotePropertyName asset_pattern -NotePropertyValue '^probe\.zip$' -Force
        $tool.needs_jdk = 25
        $script:FakeJavaHome = $java
        $script:JavaHome = $null
        Write-AtomicJson -Path (Join-Path $dest '.kit-record.json') -Text ($record | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8)
        $script:InstallState.tools = [pscustomobject]@{}
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $StatePath -Force
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $shim -Force
        $recovered = @(Install-One $tool)
        Assert-True ($recovered.Count -eq 1 -and $recovered[0].id -eq 'probe') 'A missing global state lost the recoverable per-tool record'
        Assert-True (Test-Path -LiteralPath $shim) 'A missing global state prevented cached shim repair'
        Assert-True ($script:JavaHome -eq $java) 'A cached Java tool did not repair its JDK selection'
        Assert-True ((Get-Content -LiteralPath $shim -Raw).Contains('set "JAVA_HOME=' + $java.Replace('%', '%%') + '"')) 'A repaired Java shim omitted its selected JDK'
    } finally {
        Set-Item Function:\Get-GitHubRelease -Value $savedRelease
        Set-Item Function:\Install-Jdk -Value $savedJdk
        Set-Item Function:\Save-Url -Value $savedDownload
        $tool.needs_jdk = $null
    }

    $savedZipInstall = ${function:Install-GitHubZipTool}
    try {
        $rollbackTool = [pscustomobject]@{
            id = 'rollback'; method = 'github-release'
            shims = @([pscustomobject]@{ name = 'rollback'; exe_glob = 'rollback.cmd' })
        }
        $rollbackDest = Join-Path $ToolsDir 'rollback'

        function Install-GitHubZipTool {
